| **taste** | n./v. | ★味道；品味；品尝 | It may sound surprising, but people who are supersensitive to coffee's bitter taste actually drink more of it, a new study finds. | 一项新研究发现，对咖啡苦味特别敏感的人反而喝得更多，这听起来可能有些出人意料。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · Sensitivity to Coffee |

| **relationship** | n. | ★关系；关联；亲属关系 | Gift-giving is not merely a material offering, but also an expression of emotion and a vital bond that sustains interpersonal relationships. | 赠送礼物不只是一份物质上的馈赠，也是一种情感的表达，是维系人际关系的一条重要纽带。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **exchange** | n./v. | ★交换,交流；兑换 | In traditional Chinese culture, exchanging gifts among relatives and friends is an important etiquette in interpersonal communication. | 在中国传统文化中，亲朋好友之间互赠礼物是人际交往中的一种重要礼节。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **thoughtful** | adj. | ★体贴的,关心别人的；深思的,思考的；周到的；缜密思考的；沉思的 | A thoughtfully selected gift tailored to the recipient's preferences can often deepen mutual affection. | 一份精心挑选、贴合受礼者喜好的礼物，往往能加深彼此的感情。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **tradition** | n. | ★传统,惯例；风俗；传说 | The Chinese adhere to the tradition of "mutually exchanging courtesies". | 中国人遵循"礼尚往来"的传统。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **mutual** | adj. | ★相互的；共同的 | A thoughtfully selected gift tailored to the recipient's preferences can often deepen mutual affection. | 一份精心挑选、贴合受礼者喜好的礼物，往往能加深彼此的感情。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **appropriate** | adj. | ★合适的,恰当的 | Upon receiving a gift, people will express gratitude and reciprocate with a present at an appropriate time to foster a more harmonious relationship with one another. | 接受礼物时，人们会表达谢意，并在适当的时候回赠礼物，以此来增进彼此之间的融洽关系。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **sustain** | v. | ★维持,保持；支撑；遭受；经受；供养 | Gift-giving is not merely a material offering, but also an expression of emotion and a vital bond that sustains interpersonal relationships. | 赠送礼物不只是一份物质上的馈赠，也是一种情感的表达，是维系人际关系的一条重要纽带。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |
| **tailor** | n./v. | ★裁缝；裁缝店；使适应,量身打造；定制；缝制；做裁缝 | A thoughtfully selected gift tailored to the recipient's preferences can often deepen mutual affection. | 一份精心挑选、贴合受礼者喜好的礼物，往往能加深彼此的感情。 | 2026年6月 第1套 · 礼尚往来 |

## 2025年12月


### 仔细阅读（46 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **many** | adj./pron. | ★许多的；许多 | Many drug targets in the body follow a 24-hour cycle, too. | 人体内的许多药物靶点同样遵循二十四小时的循环。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · Passage One（昼夜节律） |
| **help** | v./n. | ★帮助；有助于；帮手 | Because how the body responds to a drug can differ depending on the time it is taken, it logically follows that taking medicines at specific times could help increase their effectiveness and reduce unwanted side effects. | 既然身体对药物的反应会因服药时间的不同而有所差异，那么在特定的时间服药，按理说就能提高药效、减少不必要的副作用。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · Passage One（昼夜节律） |
| **day** | n. | ★天,日；白天；时期；全盛期 | This cycle is the reason why people rest at night and are active during the day. | 正是因为这个循环，人们才在夜里休息、白天活动。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · Passage One（昼夜节律） |
| **different** | adj. | ★不同的；各种各样的；与众不同的 | This means that the specific proteins a drug is designed to modify can react differently to the medicine over the course of a 24-hour time period. | 这意味着，药物本来要去作用的那些特定蛋白质，在一天的不同时间里对药物的反应可能是不一样的。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · Passage One（昼夜节律） |

| **park** | n./v. | ★公园；停车场；停放 | Five years ago, the city of Queens, New York, announced that it would be putting bike lanes onto a stretch of Skillman Ave, and removing 116 parking spots. | 五年前，纽约市皇后区宣布要在 Skillman 大道的一段路上划出自行车道，同时取消一百一十六个停车位。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |

| **business** | n. | ★生意,商业；事务,业务；公司,企业；职责；行业；营业额 | Cyclists loved the plan, but local business owners got very angry. | 骑车的人很喜欢这个方案，但当地的店主们非常愤怒。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |

| **already** | adv. | ★已经；早已 | "Parking here is already a nightmare," one shouted at a protest rally. | "在这里停车早就已经是一场噩梦了，"一个人抗议集会时喊道。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |

| **saw** | v. | ★看见(see 的过去式)；锯 | In the year after the bike lanes arrived, businesses on Skillman saw sales rise by 12 percent. | 自行车道修好之后的那一年，Skillman 沿街商户的营业额上升了百分之十二。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |

| **economic** | adj. | ★经济的,经济上的 | There is a rising amount of data showing that installing bike lanes and making streets more pedestrian-friendly boosts the economic fortunes of a place. | 越来越多的数据表明，修建自行车道、让街道对行人更友好，会提升一个地方的经济景气程度。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **possibility** | n. | ★可能性；可能的事 | Crises, after all, have a way of opening people's eyes to possibilities. | 危机毕竟有办法让人看到新的可能性。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **road** | n. | ★路,道路；途径；公路 | What is more, that section of road saw new businesses open. | 更重要的是，那一段路上还开了一些新店。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **spot** | n./v. | ★地点；斑点；污点；少量；发现；认出；沾污 | Five years ago, the city of Queens, New York, announced that it would be putting bike lanes onto a stretch of Skillman Ave, and removing 116 parking spots. | 五年前，纽约市皇后区宣布要在 Skillman 大道的一段路上划出自行车道，同时取消一百一十六个停车位。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **stretch** | v./n. | ★伸展,舒展；延伸；一段时间；一段路程；弹性 | Five years ago, the city of Queens, New York, announced that it would be putting bike lanes onto a stretch of Skillman Ave, and removing 116 parking spots. | 五年前，纽约市皇后区宣布要在 Skillman 大道的一段路上划出自行车道，同时取消一百一十六个停车位。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **lane** | n. | ★小路；车道；小巷；泳道 | Five years ago, the city of Queens, New York, announced that it would be putting bike lanes onto a stretch of Skillman Ave, and removing 116 parking spots. | 五年前，纽约市皇后区宣布要在 Skillman 大道的一段路上划出自行车道，同时取消一百一十六个停车位。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **fortune** | n. | ★财富；运气；命运 | There is a rising amount of data showing that installing bike lanes and making streets more pedestrian-friendly boosts the economic fortunes of a place. | 越来越多的数据表明，修建自行车道、让街道对行人更友好，会提升一个地方的经济景气程度。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **protest** | n./v. | ★抗议,反对；坚决主张 | Taking out those parking spots, as they argued at protests and in letters to the city council, would ruin stores and restaurants along Skillman. | 他们无论是在抗议现场还是在写给市议会的信里都争辩说，取消这些停车位会毁掉 Skillman 沿街的商店和餐馆。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |
| **boost** | v./n. | ★促进,提高；推动 | There is a rising amount of data showing that installing bike lanes and making streets more pedestrian-friendly boosts the economic fortunes of a place. | 越来越多的数据表明，修建自行车道、让街道对行人更友好，会提升一个地方的经济景气程度。 | 2025年12月 第1套 · The Battle over Bike Lanes |

| **talent** | n. | ★天赋,才能；人才,天才 | With those born with natural talents, it feels as if they excel without really trying. | 那些天生有才华的人，似乎不费吹灰之力就能出类拔萃。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Passage Two（天赋） |

| **life** | n. | ★生活；生命；一生；生命力；生涯 | Now that design education is limited in the curriculum, I'm all in favour of competitions such as Design Ventura, which helps to bring the standard curriculum to life, says Cleaver. | 既然设计在课程中的地位有限，我完全支持 Design Ventura 这类竞赛，它有助于让标准课程变得鲜活，克利弗说。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **come** | v. | ★来；到达；发生；变得 | A lot of research comes to the same conclusion that fruit juice does harm to the human body when it leads to surplus calories. | 大量研究得出了同样的结论：当果汁导致热量过剩时，它会对人体造成伤害。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |
| **believe** | v. | ★相信；认为；信任；信仰 | Some people believe a juice diet helps to rid our body of harmful or poisonous substances. | 有些人相信，果汁饮食有助于清除我们体内的有害或有毒物质。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |
| **visit** | v./n. | ★拜访,访问；参观；视察；探望；逗留；访问；参观；探望；出诊 | However, if these designers had visited the Design Museum shop in London earlier this year their fears may have been eased. | 不过，如果当初这些设计师去过伦敦设计博物馆的商店，他们的担忧或许就能减轻了。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **develop** | v. | ★发展；培养；开发；冲洗(照片) | It brings the business of design to life for students aged 13 to 16 by challenging them to develop a new creative, sustainable and commercially feasible product. | 它面向十三到十六岁的学生，要求他们开发一件新颖、可持续且商业上可行的产品，由此让设计这门生意变得触手可及。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **keep** | v. | ★保持,继续；保存；饲养 | In essence, each driver would constantly make adjustments to keep their car roughly halfway between the car in front and the car behind. | 本质上，每位驾驶员都要不断做出调整，让自己的车大致保持在前车与后车中间的位置。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Phantom Traffic Jams（第3套） |
| **our** | pron. | ★我们的 | Some people believe a juice diet helps to rid our body of harmful or poisonous substances. | 有些人相信，果汁饮食有助于清除我们体内的有害或有毒物质。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |
| **subject** | n./adj./v. | ★主题,题目；学科；主语；受试者；臣民；受…支配的；使服从；使经历 | Teachers like it because the skills are transferable and it helps to validate the value of design as a subject. | 老师们喜欢它，因为这些技能可以迁移，也有助于确认设计作为一门学科的价值。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **age** | n./v. | ★年龄；时代；变老 | It brings the business of design to life for students aged 13 to 16 by challenging them to develop a new creative, sustainable and commercially feasible product. | 它面向十三到十六岁的学生，要求他们开发一件新颖、可持续且商业上可行的产品，由此让设计这门生意变得触手可及。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **bring** | v. | ★带来；引起；使处于 | Now that design education is limited in the curriculum, I'm all in favour of competitions such as Design Ventura, which helps to bring the standard curriculum to life, says Cleaver. | 既然设计在课程中的地位有限，我完全支持 Design Ventura 这类竞赛，它有助于让标准课程变得鲜活，克利弗说。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **last** | adj./v. | ★最后的；上一个；持续 | These figures showed that the number of pupils that sat exams in Design and Technology fell 10 percent from 204.788 to 185.279 last year. | 这些数据显示，去年参加设计与技术课考试的学生人数下降了百分之十，从二十万四千七百八十八人降到十八万五千二百七十九人。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **lead** | v./n. | ★带领；引导；领先；导致；线索 | We find that if pupils take part in Design Ventura in Year Nine it can be the trigger which leads them to opt for it at General Certificate of Secondary Education. | 我们发现，如果学生在九年级参加 Design Ventura，这可能成为促使他们在中等教育普通证书阶段选修这门课的契机。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Getting Young Minds into Design |
| **much** | adj./adv. | ★许多的；非常；…得多 | Fruit juice is different from whole fruit in that it contains much less fibre. | 果汁与完整水果的不同之处在于，它所含的膳食纤维要少得多。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |

| **whole** | adj./n. | ★完整的,全部的；整个的；所有的；整体 | Fruit juice is different from whole fruit in that it contains much less fibre. | 果汁与完整水果的不同之处在于，它所含的膳食纤维要少得多。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |

| **cause** | n./v. | ★原因；理由；事业；引起；导致 | The exact cause of these phantom traffic jams has long remained a mystery. | 这些幽灵式堵车的确切成因，长期以来一直是个谜。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Phantom Traffic Jams（第3套） |
| **claim** | v./n. | ★声称,主张；索取；索赔 | The claimed health benefits of fruit and vegetable juices have rendered the industry very profitable. | 果蔬汁宣称的那些健康功效，让这个行业变得非常赚钱。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |
| **diet** | n. | ★饮食；节食；日常饮食 | Some people believe a juice diet helps to rid our body of harmful or poisonous substances. | 有些人相信，果汁饮食有助于清除我们体内的有害或有毒物质。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |

| **juice** | n. | ★果汁；汁液 | Fruit juice is different from whole fruit in that it contains much less fibre. | 果汁与完整水果的不同之处在于，它所含的膳食纤维要少得多。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Is Juicing Actually Good for You（第2套） |

| **until** | prep./conj. | ★直到；在…以前 | Until those systems become standard, human drivers can help reduce phantom traffic jams simply by not following the car in front so closely. | 在这些系统普及之前，驾驶员只要不紧跟前车，就能帮助减少幽灵堵塞。 | 2025年6月 第1套 · Phantom Traffic Jams（第3套） |

### 仔细阅读（117 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **how** | adv. | ★怎样；多么；如何 | Several large studies have found only a weak connection between the amount of time people spend online and how lonely they feel. | 几项大型研究发现，人们上网时间的长短与孤独感之间只有微弱的联系。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **use** | v./n. | ★使用；利用；用途 | Supermarkets that simply stopped offering free bags saw a sharper decline in plastic use than those that spent money on advertising campaigns. | 那些干脆停止提供免费袋子的超市，比花大钱做广告的超市在塑料使用量上下降得更明显。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **education** | n. | ★教育；培养；教育学 | This does not mean that education is wasted. | 这并不意味着教育白费力气。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **simply** | adv. | ★仅仅；简单地；简直 | Supermarkets that simply stopped offering free bags saw a sharper decline in plastic use than those that spent money on advertising campaigns. | 那些干脆停止提供免费袋子的超市，比花大钱做广告的超市在塑料使用量上下降得更明显。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **after** | prep./conj./adv. | ★在…之后；随后 | Users who compare their ordinary lives with the carefully selected images posted by others often conclude that their own lives are inadequate, and this feeling can persist long after they close the application. | 拿自己平常的生活与别人精心挑选后发布的照片作比较的用户，常常得出自己的生活不够好的结论，而这种感觉在他们关掉应用后还会持续很久。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **benefit** | n./v. | ★好处,利益；受益 | Those who scroll passively through other people's posts tend to report lower satisfaction, while those who send messages, share opinions and maintain contact with friends they rarely meet seem to benefit. | 被动地刷别人动态的人往往满意度更低，而那些发消息、分享观点、与难得见面的朋友保持联系的人似乎能从中受益。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **life** | n. | ★生活；生命；一生；生命力；生涯 | Users who compare their ordinary lives with the carefully selected images posted by others often conclude that their own lives are inadequate, and this feeling can persist long after they close the application. | 拿自己平常的生活与别人精心挑选后发布的照片作比较的用户，常常得出自己的生活不够好的结论，而这种感觉在他们关掉应用后还会持续很久。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **measure** | n./v. | ★措施；测量；衡量；尺度 | It means that education works best when it is combined with measures that remove the obstacles to doing the right thing. | 而是说，教育只有与消除"做正确的事"障碍的措施结合起来，才最有效。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **offer** | v./n. | ★提供；提出；报价；主动提议 | Supermarkets that simply stopped offering free bags saw a sharper decline in plastic use than those that spent money on advertising campaigns. | 那些干脆停止提供免费袋子的超市，比花大钱做广告的超市在塑料使用量上下降得更明显。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **second** | adj./n. | ★第二的；秒；瞬间 | A second factor is expectation. | 第二个因素是期望。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **habit** | n. | ★习惯,习性 | It is convenience, habit and price. | 而是方便、习惯和价格。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **online** | adj./adv. | ★在线的；网上的；在线地 | Several large studies have found only a weak connection between the amount of time people spend online and how lonely they feel. | 几项大型研究发现，人们上网时间的长短与孤独感之间只有微弱的联系。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **only** | adj./adv. | ★唯一的；仅仅；只不过 | A reusable bag works only if you remember to carry it, and a paper bag costs more and tears easily when it rains. | 可重复使用的袋子只有在你记得带上它时才起作用，而纸袋更贵，下雨时还容易破。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **rarely** | adv. | ★很少,难得；不常；异乎寻常地；极好地 | The reason is rarely ignorance. | 原因很少是无知。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **appear** | v. | ★出现；显得；似乎；出场 | What matters more appears to be how they use it. | 更关键的似乎是他们如何使用它。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **however** | adv./conj. | ★然而；无论如何 | The evidence, however, is more complicated than the claim. | 然而证据比这种说法复杂得多。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **large** | adj. | ★大的；大量的；广泛的 | Several large studies have found only a weak connection between the amount of time people spend online and how lonely they feel. | 几项大型研究发现，人们上网时间的长短与孤独感之间只有微弱的联系。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **reason** | n./v. | ★原因,理由；理性；推理 | The reason is rarely ignorance. | 原因很少是无知。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **survey** | n./v. | ★调查；勘测；测量；全面审视；概述；纵览 | Surveys repeatedly show that customers understand the environmental cost of single-use plastic, and that they still choose it when they stand at the checkout. | 调查反复显示，顾客明白一次性塑料的环境代价，可站在收银台前时他们依然选择它。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **treat** | v./n. | ★对待,看待；处理；治疗；请客；款待；乐事 | Psychologists suggest that users should treat online activity like any other part of their diet: a moderate amount, chosen deliberately, is far healthier than an endless stream of whatever appears on the screen. | 心理学家建议，用户应当像对待饮食里的其他部分一样对待线上活动：有节制、经过选择的量，比屏幕上无休止涌来的东西要健康得多。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **customer** | n. | ★顾客,客户 | Surveys repeatedly show that customers understand the environmental cost of single-use plastic, and that they still choose it when they stand at the checkout. | 调查反复显示，顾客明白一次性塑料的环境代价，可站在收银台前时他们依然选择它。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **describe** | v. | ★描述,形容 | Researchers describe this as the difference between consuming and connecting. | 研究人员把这描述为"消费"与"连接"之间的差别。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **long** | v./adj. | ★渴望；长的 | Users who compare their ordinary lives with the carefully selected images posted by others often conclude that their own lives are inadequate, and this feeling can persist long after they close the application. | 拿自己平常的生活与别人精心挑选后发布的照片作比较的用户，常常得出自己的生活不够好的结论，而这种感觉在他们关掉应用后还会持续很久。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **remove** | v. | ★移走,去掉；脱下；免职；搬家；消除 | It means that education works best when it is combined with measures that remove the obstacles to doing the right thing. | 而是说，教育只有与消除"做正确的事"障碍的措施结合起来，才最有效。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **share** | v./n. | ★分享,共享；分担；股份；份额；一份 | Those who scroll passively through other people's posts tend to report lower satisfaction, while those who send messages, share opinions and maintain contact with friends they rarely meet seem to benefit. | 被动地刷别人动态的人往往满意度更低，而那些发消息、分享观点、与难得见面的朋友保持联系的人似乎能从中受益。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **concern** | n./v. | ★关心,担忧；涉及；事项 | Researchers have long been concerned about the sleeping habits of college students. | 研究人员长期关注大学生的睡眠习惯。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **consider** | v. | ★考虑；认为 | What surprised the researchers was that many students did not consider sleep a serious issue. | 让研究人员意外的是，许多学生并不把睡眠当成严重问题。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **great** | adj. | ★伟大的；巨大的；极好的 | The trains are fast, safe and relatively comfortable, and they have greatly reduced the time needed to travel between major cities. | 这些列车快捷、安全且相对舒适，大大缩短了往来于各大城市之间所需的时间。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **network** | n./v. | ★网络；人际关系网；建立网络 | In recent years, China has built the largest high-speed railway network in the world. | 近年来，中国建成了世界上最大的高速铁路网。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **passenger** | n. | ★乘客,旅客；过路人；不干活的白吃饭的人 | Millions of passengers choose them every day, not only because they save time but also because tickets are affordable for ordinary people. | 每天有数百万旅客选择乘坐高铁，不仅因为它省时，也因为普通人也买得起车票。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **railway** | n. | ★铁路,铁道；铁路系统；铁路公司；轨道 | In recent years, China has built the largest high-speed railway network in the world. | 近年来，中国建成了世界上最大的高速铁路网。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **region** | n. | ★地区,区域；范围,领域 | The network has also promoted economic development in inland regions, where factories and tourist sites were once difficult to reach. | 高铁网还带动了内陆地区的经济发展，那里的工厂和旅游景点曾经很难到达。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **relatively** | adv. | ★相对地,比较地 | The trains are fast, safe and relatively comfortable, and they have greatly reduced the time needed to travel between major cities. | 这些列车快捷、安全且相对舒适，大大缩短了往来于各大城市之间所需的时间。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **development** | n. | ★发展；开发；进展；成长 | The network has also promoted economic development in inland regions, where factories and tourist sites were once difficult to reach. | 高铁网还带动了内陆地区的经济发展，那里的工厂和旅游景点曾经很难到达。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **economic** | adj. | ★经济的,经济上的 | The network has also promoted economic development in inland regions, where factories and tourist sites were once difficult to reach. | 高铁网还带动了内陆地区的经济发展，那里的工厂和旅游景点曾经很难到达。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |

| **construction** | n. | ★建造,建设；建筑物；结构；构造；解释 | Engineers continue to improve the technology, and several new lines are now under construction. | 工程师们不断改进这项技术，目前还有几条新线路正在建设之中。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |
| **major** | adj./n./v. | ★主要的；专业；主修；少校 | The trains are fast, safe and relatively comfortable, and they have greatly reduced the time needed to travel between major cities. | 这些列车快捷、安全且相对舒适，大大缩短了往来于各大城市之间所需的时间。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 中国高铁 |

| **tool** | n. | ★工具；手段；器械 | On the other hand, many people worry that machines will replace human workers, and that students may rely too heavily on tools that write essays for them. | 另一方面，许多人担心机器会取代人类劳动者，也担心学生会过度依赖那些替他们写文章的工具。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 范文 |

| **artificial** | adj. | ★人造的；人工的；虚假的 | Artificial intelligence is changing the way we work and study, and its influence can be seen almost everywhere. | 人工智能正在改变我们工作和学习的方式，它的影响几乎随处可见。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **assistant** | n./adj. | ★助手,助理；助教；店员；辅助的；助理的 | Schools should teach students to treat these tools as assistants rather than substitutes for thinking. | 学校应当教会学生把这些工具当作助手，而不是思维的替代品。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **handle** | v./n. | ★处理,应对；把手；柄 | On the one hand, it saves us a great deal of time by handling repetitive tasks, and it helps doctors analyse medical images more accurately. | 一方面，它通过处理重复性任务为我们节省了大量时间，还能帮助医生更准确地分析医学影像。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **view** | n./v. | ★视野；景色；观点,看法；视图；看待,考虑；观看；查看；视察 | In my view, technology itself is neither good nor bad; what matters is how we choose to use it. | 在我看来，技术本身无所谓好坏，关键在于我们选择如何使用它。 | 2024年6月 第1套 · 范文 |

### 仔细阅读（154 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |

| **has** | v./aux. | ★有(have 的第三人称单数) | Supporters of the traditional exam reply that it has one virtue no alternative has matched: it is difficult to cheat when hundreds of students answer the same questions at the same time under supervision. | 传统考试的支持者反驳说，它有一个替代方案比不了的优点：几百人在同一时间、有人监考的情况下回答同样的问题，很难作弊。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **student** | n. | ★学生；大学生；研究者 | Every summer, students sit in crowded halls and try to recall, in three hours, everything they were supposed to have learned in a semester. | 每年夏天，学生们挤在考场里，试图用三个小时回忆起一整个学期本该学会的所有东西。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **time** | n. | ★时间；次,回；时代；时机；倍数 | Supporters of the traditional exam reply that it has one virtue no alternative has matched: it is difficult to cheat when hundreds of students answer the same questions at the same time under supervision. | 传统考试的支持者反驳说，它有一个替代方案比不了的优点：几百人在同一时间、有人监考的情况下回答同样的问题，很难作弊。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **city** | n. | ★城市,都市；全市居民 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **which** | pron./adj. | ★哪一个；…的那个；关系代词 | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **should** | v./aux. | ★应该；应当；万一 | Critics argue that this ritual measures memory under pressure rather than genuine understanding, and that a single afternoon should not determine a student's grade. | 批评者认为，这种仪式考的是压力下的记忆力，而不是真正的理解，一个下午也不该决定学生的成绩。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **also** | adv. | ★也；而且；此外 | They also point out that continuous assessment places a heavy burden on teachers, who must mark far more work, and that students in such systems often complain about the constant pressure of deadlines. | 他们还指出，持续性评估给教师带来沉重负担，因为要批改的作业多得多，而且在这种制度下学生常常抱怨截止日期带来的持续压力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **now** | adv. | ★现在；此刻；如今 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **change** | n./v. | ★变化；零钱；改变；更换 | The evidence from universities that have made the change is mixed. | 已经做出改变的大学，反馈出来的证据是混杂的。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **hour** | n. | ★小时；时间,时刻；课时；固定时间 | Every summer, students sit in crowded halls and try to recall, in three hours, everything they were supposed to have learned in a semester. | 每年夏天，学生们挤在考场里，试图用三个小时回忆起一整个学期本该学会的所有东西。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **may** | v./aux. | ★可以；可能；也许 | People who already value exercise may choose to live near parks, which means the park itself may not be the cause of their better health. | 本来就重视锻炼的人可能选择住在公园附近，这意味着公园本身未必是他们更健康的原因。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **part** | n. | ★部分；角色；零件；地区；参与 | Grades tend to rise, partly because students are assessed on their best work rather than their worst day, but employers sometimes complain that they can no longer compare graduates from different institutions. | 成绩往往会上升，部分原因是学生按最好的表现而不是最糟的一天被评价，但雇主有时会抱怨他们再也无法比较不同学校的毕业生。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **project** | n./v. | ★项目,工程；课题；投射；预测；伸出 | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **short** | adj. | ★短的；矮的；短暂的；缺乏的 | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **support** | v./n. | ★支持,支撑；供养；证实；支持；支撑物；赡养 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **system** | n. | ★系统；制度；体系；方法 | They also point out that continuous assessment places a heavy burden on teachers, who must mark far more work, and that students in such systems often complain about the constant pressure of deadlines. | 他们还指出，持续性评估给教师带来沉重负担，因为要批改的作业多得多，而且在这种制度下学生常常抱怨截止日期带来的持续压力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **three** | num. | ★三；三个 | Every summer, students sit in crowded halls and try to recall, in three hours, everything they were supposed to have learned in a semester. | 每年夏天，学生们挤在考场里，试图用三个小时回忆起一整个学期本该学会的所有东西。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **traditional** | adj. | ★传统的；惯例的 | Supporters of the traditional exam reply that it has one virtue no alternative has matched: it is difficult to cheat when hundreds of students answer the same questions at the same time under supervision. | 传统考试的支持者反驳说，它有一个替代方案比不了的优点：几百人在同一时间、有人监考的情况下回答同样的问题，很难作弊。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **value** | n./v. | ★价值；价格；重要性；重视；评价 | People who already value exercise may choose to live near parks, which means the park itself may not be the cause of their better health. | 本来就重视锻炼的人可能选择住在公园附近，这意味着公园本身未必是他们更健康的原因。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **within** | prep. | ★在…之内；在…里面；不超过；在内部 | In a study that compared districts with similar income levels, residents who lived within a ten-minute walk of a park reported fewer symptoms of anxiety and were more likely to exercise regularly. | 在一项比较了收入水平相近街区的研究中，住在离公园步行十分钟范围内的居民报告说焦虑症状更少，也更可能经常锻炼。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **clear** | adj./v. | ★清楚的；明显的；清除；晴朗的 | What is less clear is the direction of the relationship. | 不那么清楚的是因果方向。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **already** | adv. | ★已经；早已 | People who already value exercise may choose to live near parks, which means the park itself may not be the cause of their better health. | 本来就重视锻炼的人可能选择住在公园附近，这意味着公园本身未必是他们更健康的原因。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **best** | adj./adv. | ★最好的；最好地 | Grades tend to rise, partly because students are assessed on their best work rather than their worst day, but employers sometimes complain that they can no longer compare graduates from different institutions. | 成绩往往会上升，部分原因是学生按最好的表现而不是最糟的一天被评价，但雇主有时会抱怨他们再也无法比较不同学校的毕业生。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **space** | n. | ★空间；太空；空地；间隔 | There is also evidence that green space reduces the temperature of dense urban areas during heat waves, and that trees filter pollutants from the air. | 也有证据表明，绿地能在热浪期间降低密集城区的温度，树木还能过滤空气中的污染物。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **growing** | adj./v. | ★增长的；生长(grow 的现在分词) | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **level** | n./adj. | ★水平；级别；层次；高度；水平的 | In a study that compared districts with similar income levels, residents who lived within a ten-minute walk of a park reported fewer symptoms of anxiety and were more likely to exercise regularly. | 在一项比较了收入水平相近街区的研究中，住在离公园步行十分钟范围内的居民报告说焦虑症状更少，也更可能经常锻炼。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **lived** | v. | ★居住(live 的过去式) | In a study that compared districts with similar income levels, residents who lived within a ten-minute walk of a park reported fewer symptoms of anxiety and were more likely to exercise regularly. | 在一项比较了收入水平相近街区的研究中，住在离公园步行十分钟范围内的居民报告说焦虑症状更少，也更可能经常锻炼。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **neighbourhood** | n. | ★街区,城区；附近；邻里；邻近地区；街坊；社区关系 | Critics warn, however, that new parks can raise property prices and push poorer residents out of the neighbourhood they were meant to serve. | 然而批评者提醒，新建公园会推高房价，把本该受益的较贫穷居民挤出这个街区。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **picture** | n./v. | ★图片；照片；想象 | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **planner** | n. | ★规划者；计划表；设计者 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **raise** | v./n. | ★提高,增加；提出；筹集；养育；举起；加薪 | Critics warn, however, that new parks can raise property prices and push poorer residents out of the neighbourhood they were meant to serve. | 然而批评者提醒，新建公园会推高房价，把本该受益的较贫穷居民挤出这个街区。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **reply** | n./v. | ★回答,答复；回应 | Supporters of the traditional exam reply that it has one virtue no alternative has matched: it is difficult to cheat when hundreds of students answer the same questions at the same time under supervision. | 传统考试的支持者反驳说，它有一个替代方案比不了的优点：几百人在同一时间、有人监考的情况下回答同样的问题，很难作弊。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **sometimes** | adv. | ★有时；间或 | Grades tend to rise, partly because students are assessed on their best work rather than their worst day, but employers sometimes complain that they can no longer compare graduates from different institutions. | 成绩往往会上升，部分原因是学生按最好的表现而不是最糟的一天被评价，但雇主有时会抱怨他们再也无法比较不同学校的毕业生。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **spread** | v./n. | ★传播；展开；蔓延 | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **tests** | n. | ★测试；考试(test 的复数) | They propose continuous assessment instead: essays, projects and short tests spread across the term, which they claim give a fairer picture of a student's ability. | 他们主张改用持续性评估：论文、项目和分散在整个学期的小测验，他们认为这样能更公平地反映学生的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **trees** | n. | ★树(tree 的复数) | There is also evidence that green space reduces the temperature of dense urban areas during heat waves, and that trees filter pollutants from the air. | 也有证据表明，绿地能在热浪期间降低密集城区的温度，树木还能过滤空气中的污染物。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **tried** | v. | ★尝试(try 的过去式)；努力 | Researchers who tried to control for this factor still found a measurable benefit, though a smaller one. | 试图控制这一因素的研究人员仍然发现了可测量的好处，只是幅度更小一些。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **warn** | v. | ★警告,提醒；告诫；预告；通知；示警 | Critics warn, however, that new parks can raise property prices and push poorer residents out of the neighbourhood they were meant to serve. | 然而批评者提醒，新建公园会推高房价，把本该受益的较贫穷居民挤出这个街区。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **challenge** | n./v. | ★挑战；质疑 | The challenge for planners, therefore, is not simply to plant more trees but to ensure that the people who need green space most can afford to stay near it. | 因此规划者面临的挑战不只是种更多的树，而是确保最需要绿地的人还能住得起附近的地方。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **combination** | n. | ★结合；组合；联合 | Perhaps the sensible conclusion is that both methods have weaknesses, and that a combination, carefully designed for each subject, serves students better than any single system. | 或许合理的结论是：两种方法都有弱点，针对每门课精心设计出来的组合，比任何单一制度都更能服务学生。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **therefore** | adv. | ★因此,所以 | The challenge for planners, therefore, is not simply to plant more trees but to ensure that the people who need green space most can afford to stay near it. | 因此规划者面临的挑战不只是种更多的树，而是确保最需要绿地的人还能住得起附近的地方。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **climate** | n. | ★气候；氛围 | The effect was modest but remarkably consistent across different cities and climates. | 这种效应不算大，但在不同城市和气候条件下都出奇地一致。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **determine** | v. | ★决定,确定；下决心 | Critics argue that this ritual measures memory under pressure rather than genuine understanding, and that a single afternoon should not determine a student's grade. | 批评者认为，这种仪式考的是压力下的记忆力，而不是真正的理解，一个下午也不该决定学生的成绩。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **suppose** | v. | ★认为,料想；假定；假定…的结果；期望 | Every summer, students sit in crowded halls and try to recall, in three hours, everything they were supposed to have learned in a semester. | 每年夏天，学生们挤在考场里，试图用三个小时回忆起一整个学期本该学会的所有东西。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **suspect** | v./n./adj. | ★怀疑,疑有；怀疑；嫌疑犯；可疑的 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **researcher** | n. | ★研究者,调查者 | Researchers who tried to control for this factor still found a measurable benefit, though a smaller one. | 试图控制这一因素的研究人员仍然发现了可测量的好处，只是幅度更小一些。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **resident** | n./adj. | ★居民,住户；住院医生；居住的 | City planners have long suspected that parks are good for residents, and a growing body of research now supports that intuition. | 城市规划者早就怀疑公园对居民有益，现在越来越多的研究支持这种直觉。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **constant** | adj./n. | ★持续的；不变的；常数 | They also point out that continuous assessment places a heavy burden on teachers, who must mark far more work, and that students in such systems often complain about the constant pressure of deadlines. | 他们还指出，持续性评估给教师带来沉重负担，因为要批改的作业多得多，而且在这种制度下学生常常抱怨截止日期带来的持续压力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **genuine** | adj. | ★真正的；真诚的 | Critics argue that this ritual measures memory under pressure rather than genuine understanding, and that a single afternoon should not determine a student's grade. | 批评者认为，这种仪式考的是压力下的记忆力，而不是真正的理解，一个下午也不该决定学生的成绩。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

### 选词填空（34 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |

| **say** | v. | ★说,讲；表明；据说 | Students who take part in community service say they gain more than they give: they learn to cooperate with people from different backgrounds, and they often discover abilities they did not know they possessed. | 参加社区服务的学生说，他们得到的比付出的更多：他们学会了与不同背景的人合作，还常常发现自己并不知道自身具备的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **day** | n. | ★天,日；白天；时期；全盛期 | Some projects last only a few days, which is too short to produce any lasting effect, and a few students admit that they join merely to add a line to their résumé. | 有些项目只持续几天，短得产生不了任何持久效果；少数学生承认，他们参加只是为了给简历添一行字。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **education** | n. | ★教育；培养；教育学 | Volunteering has become increasingly popular among young people, and universities now regard it as an important part of education. | 志愿服务在年轻人中越来越流行，大学如今也把它视为教育的重要组成部分。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **take** | v. | ★拿,取；花费(时间)；乘坐；接受 | Students who take part in community service say they gain more than they give: they learn to cooperate with people from different backgrounds, and they often discover abilities they did not know they possessed. | 参加社区服务的学生说，他们得到的比付出的更多：他们学会了与不同背景的人合作，还常常发现自己并不知道自身具备的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **course** | n. | ★课程；过程；进程；路线；一道菜 | In rural schools, for example, volunteer teachers have helped to reduce the shortage of staff and have introduced courses that the schools could not otherwise offer. | 例如在乡村学校，志愿教师帮助缓解了师资短缺，还开设了这些学校原本无法提供的课程。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **offer** | v./n. | ★提供；提出；报价；主动提议 | In rural schools, for example, volunteer teachers have helped to reduce the shortage of staff and have introduced courses that the schools could not otherwise offer. | 例如在乡村学校，志愿教师帮助缓解了师资短缺，还开设了这些学校原本无法提供的课程。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **school** | n. | ★学校；学院；上学期间；学派 | In rural schools, for example, volunteer teachers have helped to reduce the shortage of staff and have introduced courses that the schools could not otherwise offer. | 例如在乡村学校，志愿教师帮助缓解了师资短缺，还开设了这些学校原本无法提供的课程。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **university** | n. | ★大学；综合性大学 | Volunteering has become increasingly popular among young people, and universities now regard it as an important part of education. | 志愿服务在年轻人中越来越流行，大学如今也把它视为教育的重要组成部分。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **introduce** | v. | ★介绍；引进；提出；推行 | In rural schools, for example, volunteer teachers have helped to reduce the shortage of staff and have introduced courses that the schools could not otherwise offer. | 例如在乡村学校，志愿教师帮助缓解了师资短缺，还开设了这些学校原本无法提供的课程。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **young** | adj./n. | ★年轻的；幼小的；年轻人；幼崽 | Volunteering has become increasingly popular among young people, and universities now regard it as an important part of education. | 志愿服务在年轻人中越来越流行，大学如今也把它视为教育的重要组成部分。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **among** | prep. | ★在…之中；在…之间 | Volunteering has become increasingly popular among young people, and universities now regard it as an important part of education. | 志愿服务在年轻人中越来越流行，大学如今也把它视为教育的重要组成部分。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |
| **community** | n. | ★社区,群体；界 | Students who take part in community service say they gain more than they give: they learn to cooperate with people from different backgrounds, and they often discover abilities they did not know they possessed. | 参加社区服务的学生说，他们得到的比付出的更多：他们学会了与不同背景的人合作，还常常发现自己并不知道自身具备的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Volunteering among Young People |

| **pressure** | n./v. | ★压力；压迫；施加压力 | The most common reasons given were convenience and the pressure of a tight schedule, rather than the taste of the food. | 给出的最常见理由是方便和时间紧，而不是食物好吃。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **rely** | v. | ★依赖,依靠；信赖 | More than half of them order takeaway food at least three times a week, and nearly a quarter say they rely on it almost every day. | 超过一半的人每周至少点三次外卖，近四分之一的人说几乎每天都要靠外卖解决。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **said** | v. | ★说；讲(say 的过去式/过去分词) | Employers who joined the programme said graduates were generally well prepared in theory but often lacked the ability to communicate with clients. | 参加该项目的雇主表示，毕业生在理论上普遍准备充分，但往往缺乏与客户沟通的能力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **popular** | adj. | ★流行的；受欢迎的；大众的 | In recent years, tea houses have become popular among young people, who go there not only to taste tea but also to escape the pressure of a busy schedule. | 近年来，茶馆在年轻人中流行起来，他们去那里不只是为了品茶，也是为了躲避繁忙日程带来的压力。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 中国茶文化 |

### 写作（15 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **tool** | n. | ★工具；手段；器械 | In my opinion, we should treat online shopping as a tool rather than a habit. | 在我看来，我们应该把网购当作工具，而不是习惯。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **avoid** | v. | ★避免,回避 | If we shop more deliberately, we can enjoy the convenience while avoiding the regret that often follows it. | 如果我们购物时更用心一些，就能既享受便利，又避开随之而来的后悔。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **allow** | v. | ★允许；准许；给予；考虑到 | It allows consumers to compare prices within minutes, and goods are delivered to their door, which saves a great deal of time. | 它让消费者在几分钟内就能比较价格，商品直接送到家门口，省下大量时间。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **goods** | n. | ★商品；货物 | It allows consumers to compare prices within minutes, and goods are delivered to their door, which saves a great deal of time. | 它让消费者在几分钟内就能比较价格，商品直接送到家门口，省下大量时间。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **deliver** | v. | ★递送；发表；接生；实现(承诺) | It allows consumers to compare prices within minutes, and goods are delivered to their door, which saves a great deal of time. | 它让消费者在几分钟内就能比较价格，商品直接送到家门口，省下大量时间。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **necessary** | adj. | ★必要的；必需的 | Before placing an order, we ought to ask ourselves whether the purchase is really necessary. | 下单之前，我们应当问问自己这次购买是否真的有必要。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **packaging** | n. | ★包装；包装材料 | Customers often buy things they do not need simply because they are cheap, and the packaging creates a large amount of waste. | 顾客常常仅仅因为便宜就买下并不需要的东西，而包装又制造了大量垃圾。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **really** | adv. | ★真正地；确实；非常 | Before placing an order, we ought to ask ourselves whether the purchase is really necessary. | 下单之前，我们应当问问自己这次购买是否真的有必要。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **saves** | v. | ★节省(save 的第三人称单数) | It allows consumers to compare prices within minutes, and goods are delivered to their door, which saves a great deal of time. | 它让消费者在几分钟内就能比较价格，商品直接送到家门口，省下大量时间。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **advantage** | n. | ★优势,好处 | Online shopping has become part of daily life for most people, and its advantages are obvious. | 网购已经成为大多数人日常生活的一部分，它的优势显而易见。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **obvious** | adj. | ★明显的,显而易见的 | Online shopping has become part of daily life for most people, and its advantages are obvious. | 网购已经成为大多数人日常生活的一部分，它的优势显而易见。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **regret** | n./v. | ★后悔,遗憾；抱歉；懊悔 | If we shop more deliberately, we can enjoy the convenience while avoiding the regret that often follows it. | 如果我们购物时更用心一些，就能既享受便利，又避开随之而来的后悔。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **deliberately** | adv. | ★故意地；从容不迫地；深思熟虑地；谨慎地 | If we shop more deliberately, we can enjoy the convenience while avoiding the regret that often follows it. | 如果我们购物时更用心一些，就能既享受便利，又避开随之而来的后悔。 | 2023年12月 第1套 · 范文 |

| **some** | adj./pron. | ★一些；某个；大约 | Teachers report that some students now find it difficult to concentrate for more than a few minutes, and that they abandon a text as soon as it stops being immediately interesting. | 教师反映，一些学生现在很难集中注意力超过几分钟，一段文字一旦不再立刻有趣，他们就放弃了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **hour** | n. | ★小时；时间,时刻；课时；固定时间 | This problem can be managed by reserving certain hours for research. | 这个问题可以通过保留某些时段专供研究来解决。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **part** | n. | ★部分；角色；零件；地区；参与 | Yet several museums have found that visitors who enter for free spend more in the shop and café, and that donations from satisfied visitors partly replace the lost ticket revenue. | 不过几家博物馆发现，免费进来的访客在商店和咖啡馆消费更多，满意访客的捐赠也部分弥补了门票收入的损失。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **few** | adj./pron. | ★很少的；不多的；几个 | Teachers report that some students now find it difficult to concentrate for more than a few minutes, and that they abandon a text as soon as it stops being immediately interesting. | 教师反映，一些学生现在很难集中注意力超过几分钟，一段文字一旦不再立刻有趣，他们就放弃了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **require** | v. | ★需要；要求,规定 | Short videos have become the dominant form of online entertainment, and their appeal is easy to explain: they require almost no effort, they deliver a reward within seconds, and the next one begins before you have decided whether you want it. | 短视频已经成为网络娱乐的主要形式，它的吸引力不难解释：几乎不费力气，几秒钟内就给到回报，而且你还没决定要不要看，下一条已经开始了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **several** | adj./pron. | ★几个；若干 | Yet several museums have found that visitors who enter for free spend more in the shop and café, and that donations from satisfied visitors partly replace the lost ticket revenue. | 不过几家博物馆发现，免费进来的访客在商店和咖啡馆消费更多，满意访客的捐赠也部分弥补了门票收入的损失。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **far** | adv./adj. | ★远；很,非常；远的 | The danger lies in the second habit, and it is a habit that is far easier to acquire than to break. | 危险在于第二种习惯，而这种习惯养成容易、戒掉难。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **new** | adj. | ★新的；新鲜的；新来的 | Countries that abolished entrance fees recorded a sharp increase in visitors, and, crucially, the new visitors were not the usual audience. | 取消门票的国家录得访客量急剧上升，而且关键是，新增的访客并不是原来那批老观众。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **problem** | n. | ★问题,难题；习题 | This problem can be managed by reserving certain hours for research. | 这个问题可以通过保留某些时段专供研究来解决。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **teacher** | n. | ★教师,老师；导师 | Teachers report that some students now find it difficult to concentrate for more than a few minutes, and that they abandon a text as soon as it stops being immediately interesting. | 教师反映，一些学生现在很难集中注意力超过几分钟，一段文字一旦不再立刻有趣，他们就放弃了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **different** | adj. | ★不同的；各种各样的；与众不同的 | Watching a video that requires you to think is very different from passively consuming an endless stream of clips chosen by an algorithm. | 观看一个需要你动脑的视频，与被动消费由算法推送的无尽片段，完全是两回事。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **seem** | v. | ★似乎,好像；看来 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **public** | adj./n. | ★公众的,公共的；公开的；公众,民众 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **reading** | n. | ★阅读；读物；读数 | Psychologists warn that repeated exposure to this pattern trains the brain to expect constant novelty, which makes slower activities, such as reading a book or listening to a lecture, feel uncomfortable by comparison. | 心理学家警告说，反复接触这种模式会训练大脑期待持续的新鲜感，相比之下，读书或听讲座这类较慢的活动就会让人觉得不舒服。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **book** | n./v. | ★书；书籍；预订 | Psychologists warn that repeated exposure to this pattern trains the brain to expect constant novelty, which makes slower activities, such as reading a book or listening to a lecture, feel uncomfortable by comparison. | 心理学家警告说，反复接触这种模式会训练大脑期待持续的新鲜感，相比之下，读书或听讲座这类较慢的活动就会让人觉得不舒服。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **evidence** | n. | ★证据,迹象 | On the whole, the evidence favours opening the doors: a museum that is visited by the whole community is more likely to be defended by that community when budgets are cut. | 总的来看，证据支持敞开大门：一个被整个社区参观的博物馆，在预算被削减时更可能被这个社区站出来保护。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **short** | adj. | ★短的；矮的；短暂的；缺乏的 | Short videos have become the dominant form of online entertainment, and their appeal is easy to explain: they require almost no effort, they deliver a reward within seconds, and the next one begins before you have decided whether you want it. | 短视频已经成为网络娱乐的主要形式，它的吸引力不难解释：几乎不费力气，几秒钟内就给到回报，而且你还没决定要不要看，下一条已经开始了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **value** | n./v. | ★价值；价格；重要性；重视；评价 | Defenders of the medium reply that short videos have their own value: they can spread knowledge quickly, and a well-made clip can explain a difficult concept more clearly than a page of prose. | 为这种媒介辩护的人回应说，短视频自有其价值：它能快速传播知识，一个制作精良的短片能把难懂的概念讲得比一页文字更清楚。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **charge** | n./v. | ★费用；负责；充电；指控；收费 | When a museum charges for admission, it sends a message about who the institution is for. | 当一座博物馆收取门票时，它其实传递了一个信息：这个机构是为谁而设的。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **community** | n. | ★社区,群体；界 | On the whole, the evidence favours opening the doors: a museum that is visited by the whole community is more likely to be defended by that community when budgets are cut. | 总的来看，证据支持敞开大门：一个被整个社区参观的博物馆，在预算被削减时更可能被这个社区站出来保护。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **never** | adv. | ★从不；决不 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **object** | n./v. | ★物体；目标；宾语；反对 | Critics object that free admission reduces the income available for maintaining collections, and there is some truth in this. | 批评者反对说，免费入场会减少用于维护藏品的收入，这话有一定道理。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **expect** | v. | ★期望；预料；认为；要求 | Psychologists warn that repeated exposure to this pattern trains the brain to expect constant novelty, which makes slower activities, such as reading a book or listening to a lecture, feel uncomfortable by comparison. | 心理学家警告说，反复接触这种模式会训练大脑期待持续的新鲜感，相比之下，读书或听讲座这类较慢的活动就会让人觉得不舒服。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **message** | n./v. | ★消息；信息；留言 | When a museum charges for admission, it sends a message about who the institution is for. | 当一座博物馆收取门票时，它其实传递了一个信息：这个机构是为谁而设的。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **minutes** | n. | ★分钟；片刻；会议记录 | Teachers report that some students now find it difficult to concentrate for more than a few minutes, and that they abandon a text as soon as it stops being immediately interesting. | 教师反映，一些学生现在很难集中注意力超过几分钟，一段文字一旦不再立刻有趣，他们就放弃了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **quickly** | adv. | ★迅速地；快地 | Defenders of the medium reply that short videos have their own value: they can spread knowledge quickly, and a well-made clip can explain a difficult concept more clearly than a page of prose. | 为这种媒介辩护的人回应说，短视频自有其价值：它能快速传播知识，一个制作精良的短片能把难懂的概念讲得比一页文字更清楚。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **repeat** | v./n. | ★重复；重说；重做；重播 | Psychologists warn that repeated exposure to this pattern trains the brain to expect constant novelty, which makes slower activities, such as reading a book or listening to a lecture, feel uncomfortable by comparison. | 心理学家警告说，反复接触这种模式会训练大脑期待持续的新鲜感，相比之下，读书或听讲座这类较慢的活动就会让人觉得不舒服。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **stream** | n./v. | ★小河,溪流；流,一股；潮流；流动；流出；飘扬；按能力分班 | Watching a video that requires you to think is very different from passively consuming an endless stream of clips chosen by an algorithm. | 观看一个需要你动脑的视频，与被动消费由算法推送的无尽片段，完全是两回事。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **usual** | adj. | ★通常的,惯例的；平常的 | Countries that abolished entrance fees recorded a sharp increase in visitors, and, crucially, the new visitors were not the usual audience. | 取消门票的国家录得访客量急剧上升，而且关键是，新增的访客并不是原来那批老观众。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **low** | adj./adv. | ★低的；矮的；低级的；低声地 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **reward** | n./v. | ★奖励,报酬；报答；奖赏 | Short videos have become the dominant form of online entertainment, and their appeal is easy to explain: they require almost no effort, they deliver a reward within seconds, and the next one begins before you have decided whether you want it. | 短视频已经成为网络娱乐的主要形式，它的吸引力不难解释：几乎不费力气，几秒钟内就给到回报，而且你还没决定要不要看，下一条已经开始了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **satisfy** | v. | ★满足,使满意；使确信；符合 | Yet several museums have found that visitors who enter for free spend more in the shop and café, and that donations from satisfied visitors partly replace the lost ticket revenue. | 不过几家博物馆发现，免费进来的访客在商店和咖啡馆消费更多，满意访客的捐赠也部分弥补了门票收入的损失。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **watch** | v./n. | ★观看,注视；观察；看守；留意；当心；手表；监视；值班人员；注意 | Watching a video that requires you to think is very different from passively consuming an endless stream of clips chosen by an algorithm. | 观看一个需要你动脑的视频，与被动消费由算法推送的无尽片段，完全是两回事。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **abandon** | v. | ★放弃,中止；抛弃,遗弃；陷入(某种情绪) | Teachers report that some students now find it difficult to concentrate for more than a few minutes, and that they abandon a text as soon as it stops being immediately interesting. | 教师反映，一些学生现在很难集中注意力超过几分钟，一段文字一旦不再立刻有趣，他们就放弃了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **modest** | adj. | ★谦虚的；适度的；朴素的 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **precise** | adj. | ★精确的,准确的；恰好的 | A modest fee may seem harmless, but research consistently shows that it discourages precisely the visitors that public funding was intended to attract: families with low incomes, students, and people who have never visited a museum before and are unsure whether they would enjoy it. | 不算高的票价看起来无伤大雅，但研究一致表明，它劝退的恰恰是公共资金本想吸引的那批访客：低收入家庭、学生，以及那些从未进过博物馆、不确定自己会不会喜欢的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **acquire** | v. | ★获得,习得(技能/语言) | The danger lies in the second habit, and it is a habit that is far easier to acquire than to break. | 危险在于第二种习惯，而这种习惯养成容易、戒掉难。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **appeal** | v./n. | ★呼吁,恳求；吸引力；上诉 | Short videos have become the dominant form of online entertainment, and their appeal is easy to explain: they require almost no effort, they deliver a reward within seconds, and the next one begins before you have decided whether you want it. | 短视频已经成为网络娱乐的主要形式，它的吸引力不难解释：几乎不费力气，几秒钟内就给到回报，而且你还没决定要不要看，下一条已经开始了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **these** | adj./pron. | ★这些 | Surveys indicate that a significant proportion of people over sixty-five feel anxious when they are asked to use an unfamiliar application, and that many of them depend on relatives to complete these tasks. | 调查显示，六十五岁以上的人中有相当大一部分在被要求使用陌生的应用时感到焦虑，其中许多人要靠亲属来完成这些事。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |
| **book** | n./v. | ★书；书籍；预订 | Booking a hospital appointment, paying a bill or even buying a ticket may now require a smartphone and a certain amount of patience. | 预约挂号、缴账单，甚至买一张票，现在都可能需要一部智能手机和相当的耐心。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |
| **amount** | n./v. | ★数量,总额；总计 | Booking a hospital appointment, paying a bill or even buying a ticket may now require a smartphone and a certain amount of patience. | 预约挂号、缴账单，甚至买一张票，现在都可能需要一部智能手机和相当的耐心。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |
| **enjoy** | v. | ★享受,喜爱；拥有 | Those who cannot manage digital services tend to withdraw from activities they once enjoyed, and this isolation is associated with poorer health. | 那些应付不了数字服务的人往往会退出以前喜欢的活动，而这种孤立与健康状况变差有关。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |

| **carry** | v. | ★携带；搬运；传播；支撑；具有；刊登；怀(孕) | As services move online, older citizens are finding it harder to carry out ordinary tasks. | 随着各种服务搬到线上，老年公民发现连办普通的事都变难了。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |

| **free** | adj./v. | ★免费的；自由的；空闲的；释放 | Several cities have responded with free training classes, where volunteers teach basic skills at a slow pace. | 一些城市的应对办法是开设免费培训班，由志愿者以很慢的节奏教基本操作。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |
| **significant** | adj. | ★重要的,重大的；显著的；意味深长的 | Surveys indicate that a significant proportion of people over sixty-five feel anxious when they are asked to use an unfamiliar application, and that many of them depend on relatives to complete these tasks. | 调查显示，六十五岁以上的人中有相当大一部分在被要求使用陌生的应用时感到焦虑，其中许多人要靠亲属来完成这些事。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · The Digital Divide among Older People |

### 听力（40 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **park** | n./v. | ★公园；停车场；停放 | A railway line that had been abandoned for more than twenty years has been converted into a public park, and local residents have welcomed the change. | 一条已经废弃二十多年的铁路线被改造成了公园，当地居民对这一变化表示欢迎。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **thousand** | num./n. | ★一千；成千上万 | City officials say the park now attracts thousands of visitors every weekend, and that small businesses near the entrances have reported a noticeable increase in income. | 市政官员说，这个公园现在每个周末吸引数千名游客，入口附近的小商家也反映收入明显增加。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **visitor** | n. | ★参观者,访问者；游客；来访者 | City officials say the park now attracts thousands of visitors every weekend, and that small businesses near the entrances have reported a noticeable increase in income. | 市政官员说，这个公园现在每个周末吸引数千名游客，入口附近的小商家也反映收入明显增加。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **tool** | n. | ★工具；手段；器械 | Teachers who use such tools say they save several hours a week, which they can spend talking to students instead of correcting papers. | 使用这类工具的教师说，它们每周为他们省下几个小时，这些时间可以用来和学生交流，而不是批改作业。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **waste** | n./v./adj. | ★浪费；废物,废料；荒地；消耗；浪费,滥用；使荒芜；废弃的；荒芜的 | The project cost relatively little, because the track and the bridges were already in place; workers mainly removed the waste and planted trees along the route. | 这个项目花费相对较少，因为铁轨和桥梁本来就在，工人主要是清理垃圾并沿线路种树。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **keep** | v. | ★保持,继续；保存；饲养 | The sensible approach, most educators agree, is to let the software do what it does well and to keep the human part of teaching where it belongs. | 大多数教育者认同的稳妥做法是：让软件去做它擅长的事，把教学中属于人的部分留在人手里。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **two** | num. | ★二；两个 | The city plans to extend the park by another two kilometres next year, although some residents worry that the noise will disturb the peace of the area. | 该市计划明年再把公园延长两公里，尽管一些居民担心噪音会打破这一带的宁静。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **another** | adj./pron. | ★另一个；再一个 | The city plans to extend the park by another two kilometres next year, although some residents worry that the noise will disturb the peace of the area. | 该市计划明年再把公园延长两公里，尽管一些居民担心噪音会打破这一带的宁静。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **mark** | v./n. | ★标记；分数；标志；痕迹 | Software can now mark multiple-choice tests instantly, analyse a student's mistakes and recommend exercises that target exactly the points the student has not understood. | 现在的软件可以瞬间批改选择题、分析学生的错误，并推荐正好针对该生没弄懂的知识点设计的练习。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **noise** | n. | ★噪音,噪声；喧闹声；杂音 | The city plans to extend the park by another two kilometres next year, although some residents worry that the noise will disturb the peace of the area. | 该市计划明年再把公园延长两公里，尽管一些居民担心噪音会打破这一带的宁静。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **person** | n. | ★人；个人；身体；外表；人称 | Experienced teachers insist that learning is a social process, and that no machine can replace the encouragement a student receives from a person who knows them. | 有经验的教师坚持认为，学习是一种社会过程，任何机器都无法取代学生从一个了解他们的人那里得到的鼓励。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **social** | adj. | ★社会的；社交的；群居的 | Experienced teachers insist that learning is a social process, and that no machine can replace the encouragement a student receives from a person who knows them. | 有经验的教师坚持认为，学习是一种社会过程，任何机器都无法取代学生从一个了解他们的人那里得到的鼓励。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **worry** | v./n. | ★担心,发愁；烦恼 | The city plans to extend the park by another two kilometres next year, although some residents worry that the noise will disturb the peace of the area. | 该市计划明年再把公园延长两公里，尽管一些居民担心噪音会打破这一带的宁静。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **analyse** | v. | ★分析；解析 | Software can now mark multiple-choice tests instantly, analyse a student's mistakes and recommend exercises that target exactly the points the student has not understood. | 现在的软件可以瞬间批改选择题、分析学生的错误，并推荐正好针对该生没弄懂的知识点设计的练习。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **answer** | n./v. | ★回答；答案；解答 | A program may identify that an answer is wrong, but it cannot explain why a student chose it, and it certainly cannot notice that a child is tired, anxious or simply bored. | 一个程序可以判断某个答案是错的，却解释不了学生为什么选它，更不可能注意到孩子是累了、焦虑还是单纯的厌烦。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **entrance** | n. | ★入口；进入；入学 | City officials say the park now attracts thousands of visitors every weekend, and that small businesses near the entrances have reported a noticeable increase in income. | 市政官员说，这个公园现在每个周末吸引数千名游客，入口附近的小商家也反映收入明显增加。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **mistakes** | n. | ★错误；失误(mistake 的复数) | Software can now mark multiple-choice tests instantly, analyse a student's mistakes and recommend exercises that target exactly the points the student has not understood. | 现在的软件可以瞬间批改选择题、分析学生的错误，并推荐正好针对该生没弄懂的知识点设计的练习。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **process** | n./v. | ★过程,进程；工序；加工；处理 | Experienced teachers insist that learning is a social process, and that no machine can replace the encouragement a student receives from a person who knows them. | 有经验的教师坚持认为，学习是一种社会过程，任何机器都无法取代学生从一个了解他们的人那里得到的鼓励。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **route** | n. | ★路线；途径；航线 | The project cost relatively little, because the track and the bridges were already in place; workers mainly removed the waste and planted trees along the route. | 这个项目花费相对较少，因为铁轨和桥梁本来就在，工人主要是清理垃圾并沿线路种树。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **wrong** | adj./adv./n. | ★错误的,不正确的；不道德的；有毛病的；错误地；不公正；冤枉 | A program may identify that an answer is wrong, but it cannot explain why a student chose it, and it certainly cannot notice that a child is tired, anxious or simply bored. | 一个程序可以判断某个答案是错的，却解释不了学生为什么选它，更不可能注意到孩子是累了、焦虑还是单纯的厌烦。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **area** | n. | ★地区；面积；领域；范围 | The city plans to extend the park by another two kilometres next year, although some residents worry that the noise will disturb the peace of the area. | 该市计划明年再把公园延长两公里，尽管一些居民担心噪音会打破这一带的宁静。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **green** | adj./n. | ★绿色的；环保的；绿色 | Similar projects in other countries suggest that the benefits last: once a green space is established, the surrounding neighbourhood tends to become more attractive to residents and investors alike. | 其他国家的类似项目表明，这些好处是持久的：一旦绿地建起来，周边街区对居民和投资者的吸引力往往都会提高。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **establish** | v. | ★建立,创立；确定；查明 | Similar projects in other countries suggest that the benefits last: once a green space is established, the surrounding neighbourhood tends to become more attractive to residents and investors alike. | 其他国家的类似项目表明，这些好处是持久的：一旦绿地建起来，周边街区对居民和投资者的吸引力往往都会提高。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **identify** | v. | ★识别,确认；认同；发现 | A program may identify that an answer is wrong, but it cannot explain why a student chose it, and it certainly cannot notice that a child is tired, anxious or simply bored. | 一个程序可以判断某个答案是错的，却解释不了学生为什么选它，更不可能注意到孩子是累了、焦虑还是单纯的厌烦。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **machine** | n. | ★机器；机械装置 | Experienced teachers insist that learning is a social process, and that no machine can replace the encouragement a student receives from a person who knows them. | 有经验的教师坚持认为，学习是一种社会过程，任何机器都无法取代学生从一个了解他们的人那里得到的鼓励。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **program** | n./v. | ★计划,方案；节目；程序；编程 | A program may identify that an answer is wrong, but it cannot explain why a student chose it, and it certainly cannot notice that a child is tired, anxious or simply bored. | 一个程序可以判断某个答案是错的，却解释不了学生为什么选它，更不可能注意到孩子是累了、焦虑还是单纯的厌烦。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **tired** | adj. | ★疲劳的,疲倦的；厌倦的；陈旧的；破旧的；厌烦的 | A program may identify that an answer is wrong, but it cannot explain why a student chose it, and it certainly cannot notice that a child is tired, anxious or simply bored. | 一个程序可以判断某个答案是错的，却解释不了学生为什么选它，更不可能注意到孩子是累了、焦虑还是单纯的厌烦。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **overlook** | v. | ★忽视,忽略；俯视；宽恕 | Yet the technology has limits that are easy to overlook. | 然而这项技术也有容易被忽视的局限。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **chinese** | adj./n. | ★中国的；中文的；中国人；汉语 | Chinese calligraphy is a traditional art that has been practised for more than two thousand years. | 中国书法是一门已经流传两千多年的传统艺术。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 中国书法 |
| **believe** | v. | ★相信；认为；信任；信仰 | Experts believe that this traditional art will continue to develop as long as it is passed on to younger generations. | 专家认为，只要这门传统艺术能传给年轻一代，它就会继续发展下去。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 中国书法 |
| **bring** | v. | ★带来；引起；使处于 | People who practise calligraphy say that it requires patience and concentration, and that the process itself brings a sense of peace. | 练习书法的人说，它需要耐心和专注，而书写的过程本身会带来一种平静感。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 中国书法 |
| **merely** | adv. | ★仅仅,只不过 | It is not merely a way of writing characters but a form of artistic expression, in which the strength and rhythm of each stroke reveal the writer's mood and personality. | 它不仅仅是一种书写汉字的方式，更是一种艺术表达形式，每一笔的力度与节奏都透露出书写者的情绪与个性。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 中国书法 |
| **mood** | n. | ★心情,情绪；语气 | It is not merely a way of writing characters but a form of artistic expression, in which the strength and rhythm of each stroke reveal the writer's mood and personality. | 它不仅仅是一种书写汉字的方式，更是一种艺术表达形式，每一笔的力度与节奏都透露出书写者的情绪与个性。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 中国书法 |

| **hobby** | n. | ★爱好；业余爱好 | A hobby may seem like a small thing in a busy life, but I believe it matters more than many people realise. | 在忙碌的生活里，爱好似乎是件小事，但我认为它的意义比许多人以为的要大。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **fail** | v. | ★失败；不及格；未能；衰退 | Second, it allows us to develop abilities that our formal education ignores, such as patience, creativity and the courage to fail in public. | 其次，它让我们发展正规教育所忽视的能力，比如耐心、创造力，以及当众出丑的勇气。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **thing** | n. | ★东西；事情；物品 | A hobby may seem like a small thing in a busy life, but I believe it matters more than many people realise. | 在忙碌的生活里，爱好似乎是件小事，但我认为它的意义比许多人以为的要大。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **finally** | adv. | ★最后；终于；最终 | Finally, hobbies often bring us into contact with people we would never otherwise meet. | 最后，爱好常常让我们接触到平时根本不会遇到的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **otherwise** | adv./conj. | ★否则；除此以外；以别的方式 | Finally, hobbies often bring us into contact with people we would never otherwise meet. | 最后，爱好常常让我们接触到平时根本不会遇到的人。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **formal** | adj. | ★正式的；形式上的 | Second, it allows us to develop abilities that our formal education ignores, such as patience, creativity and the courage to fail in public. | 其次，它让我们发展正规教育所忽视的能力，比如耐心、创造力，以及当众出丑的勇气。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **burden** | n./v. | ★负担,重担；使负重担 | Of course, a hobby should not become another burden. | 当然，爱好不该变成另一种负担。 | 2023年6月 第1套 · 范文 |

### 仔细阅读（122 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **year** | n. | ★年；年份；学年；岁 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **become** | v. | ★变成,成为；变得；适合 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **many** | adj./pron. | ★许多的；许多 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **now** | adv. | ★现在；此刻；如今 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **some** | adj./pron. | ★一些；某个；大约 | Some weather services have begun to experiment with clearer wording, and early results are encouraging. | 一些气象机构已经开始尝试更清楚的表述，初步结果令人鼓舞。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **part** | n. | ★部分；角色；零件；地区；参与 | Yet surveys repeatedly show that a large part of the public does not trust the forecast, and the reasons have less to do with science than with communication. | 然而调查反复显示，相当一部分公众并不相信预报，原因与其说和科学有关，不如说和表达有关。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **say** | v. | ★说,讲；表明；据说 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **few** | adj./pron. | ★很少的；不多的；几个 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **learn** | v. | ★学习；得知；记住 | Companies that wish to attract young talent would do better to offer predictable schedules and genuine opportunities to learn than to compete on salary alone. | 想吸引年轻人才的公司，与其只在薪资上竞争，不如提供可预期的作息和真正的学习机会。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **suggest** | v. | ★建议,提议；暗示；表明；使想起 | Recent surveys suggest that the mood has shifted. | 最近的调查显示，风向变了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **often** | adv. | ★经常；常常 | Forecasters have been slow to explain probability in ordinary language, and television presenters often simplify the information until the uncertainty disappears. | 预报员一直不擅长用日常语言解释概率，电视主持人又常常把信息简化得连不确定性都消失了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **been** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去分词) | Forecasters have been slow to explain probability in ordinary language, and television presenters often simplify the information until the uncertainty disappears. | 预报员一直不擅长用日常语言解释概率，电视主持人又常常把信息简化得连不确定性都消失了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **feel** | v. | ★感觉,觉得；认为；触摸 | In such circumstances, a familiar job where the rules are known feels less like a trap and more like a form of security. | 在这种情况下，一份规则清楚的熟悉工作，感觉就不像牢笼，而像一种安全感。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **remain** | v. | ★保持,仍然是；留下；剩余；尚待 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **way** | n./adv. | ★方法,方式；道路；方向；路线；方面；情况；习惯；远远地,大大地；非常 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **understand** | v. | ★理解,懂得；认为；体谅 | A three-day forecast today is as accurate as a one-day forecast was thirty years ago, thanks to better instruments, more powerful computers and a deeper understanding of the atmosphere. | 借助更好的仪器、更强大的计算机和对大气更深入的认识，如今三天的预报已经和三十年前一天的预报一样准。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **life** | n. | ★生活；生命；一生；生命力；生涯 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **offer** | v./n. | ★提供；提出；报价；主动提议 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **show** | v./n. | ★显示,表明；展示；演出 | Yet surveys repeatedly show that a large part of the public does not trust the forecast, and the reasons have less to do with science than with communication. | 然而调查反复显示，相当一部分公众并不相信预报，原因与其说和科学有关，不如说和表达有关。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **almost** | adv. | ★几乎；差不多 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **cost** | v./n. | ★花费；使付出；成本；代价 | Workers still care about pay, but they also weigh the cost of constant uncertainty, and increasingly they conclude that the price is too high. | 员工仍然在意薪水，但他们也会衡量持续不确定性带来的代价，而且越来越多的人认为这个代价太高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **does** | v. | ★做(do 的第三人称单数)；助动词 | Yet surveys repeatedly show that a large part of the public does not trust the forecast, and the reasons have less to do with science than with communication. | 然而调查反复显示，相当一部分公众并不相信预报，原因与其说和科学有关，不如说和表达有关。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **job** | n. | ★工作；职业；职责；活儿 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **only** | adj./adv. | ★唯一的；仅仅；只不过 | Employers who assume that money is the only factor misunderstand what has changed. | 以为钱是唯一因素的雇主，误解了变化所在。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **seem** | v. | ★似乎,好像；看来 | Trust, it seems, depends less on being right than on being understood. | 看来，信任与其说取决于说得准，不如说取决于说得让人懂。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **yet** | adv./conj. | ★然而；还；但是 | Yet surveys repeatedly show that a large part of the public does not trust the forecast, and the reasons have less to do with science than with communication. | 然而调查反复显示，相当一部分公众并不相信预报，原因与其说和科学有关，不如说和表达有关。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **still** | adv./adj. | ★仍然；还；静止的 | Workers still care about pay, but they also weigh the cost of constant uncertainty, and increasingly they conclude that the price is too high. | 员工仍然在意薪水，但他们也会衡量持续不确定性带来的代价，而且越来越多的人认为这个代价太高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **describe** | v. | ★描述,形容 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **information** | n. | ★信息,资料；情报；通知 | Forecasters have been slow to explain probability in ordinary language, and television presenters often simplify the information until the uncertainty disappears. | 预报员一直不擅长用日常语言解释概率，电视主持人又常常把信息简化得连不确定性都消失了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **money** | n. | ★钱,货币；财富；收入 | Employers who assume that money is the only factor misunderstand what has changed. | 以为钱是唯一因素的雇主，误解了变化所在。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **out** | adv. | ★出去；在外；熄灭 | Scientists, meanwhile, mean something precise: that under similar conditions in the past, it rained three times out of ten. | 而科学家的意思很确切：在过去类似的气象条件下，十次里有三次下了雨。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **project** | n./v. | ★项目,工程；课题；投射；预测；伸出 | Many describe a working environment that has become unpredictable: projects appear without warning, meetings are scheduled late in the evening, and the boundary between work and private life has almost disappeared. | 许多人描述的工作环境变得难以预测：项目说来就来，会议安排在深夜，工作与私人生活之间的界限几乎消失。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **young** | adj./n. | ★年轻的；幼小的；年轻人；幼崽 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **percent** | n. | ★百分比 | When a forecast states that there is a thirty percent chance of rain, many listeners interpret this as a promise that it will not rain, and feel cheated if it does. | 当预报说有百分之三十的概率下雨时，许多听众把它理解成"不会下雨"的承诺，一旦下了雨就觉得被骗了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **receive** | v. | ★收到,接到；受到；接待,接见 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **weather** | n./v. | ★天气,气象；处境；气候；经历；度过；经受住；风化；侵蚀 | Weather forecasting has improved dramatically. | 天气预报的准确性已经大幅提高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **current** | adj./n. | ★当前的；流行的；水流,电流 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **familiar** | adj. | ★熟悉的；常见的；随便的 | In such circumstances, a familiar job where the rules are known feels less like a trap and more like a form of security. | 在这种情况下，一份规则清楚的熟悉工作，感觉就不像牢笼，而像一种安全感。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **science** | n. | ★科学；理科；学科 | Yet surveys repeatedly show that a large part of the public does not trust the forecast, and the reasons have less to do with science than with communication. | 然而调查反复显示，相当一部分公众并不相信预报，原因与其说和科学有关，不如说和表达有关。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **trap** | n./v. | ★陷阱,圈套；困境；使陷入；使困住；设陷阱捕捉 | In such circumstances, a familiar job where the rules are known feels less like a trap and more like a form of security. | 在这种情况下，一份规则清楚的熟悉工作，感觉就不像牢笼，而像一种安全感。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **weigh** | v. | ★称重；有…重；权衡；考虑；重压；起锚；称…的重量；影响 | Workers still care about pay, but they also weigh the cost of constant uncertainty, and increasingly they conclude that the price is too high. | 员工仍然在意薪水，但他们也会衡量持续不确定性带来的代价，而且越来越多的人认为这个代价太高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **constant** | adj./n. | ★持续的；不变的；常数 | Workers still care about pay, but they also weigh the cost of constant uncertainty, and increasingly they conclude that the price is too high. | 员工仍然在意薪水，但他们也会衡量持续不确定性带来的代价，而且越来越多的人认为这个代价太高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **employer** | n. | ★雇主,老板 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **proportion** | n. | ★比例,部分；均衡；面积 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **forecast** | n./v. | ★预报,预测 | Weather forecasting has improved dramatically. | 天气预报的准确性已经大幅提高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **precise** | adj. | ★精确的,准确的；恰好的 | Scientists, meanwhile, mean something precise: that under similar conditions in the past, it rained three times out of ten. | 而科学家的意思很确切：在过去类似的气象条件下，十次里有三次下了雨。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **employee** | n. | ★雇员,员工 | A large proportion of employees under thirty now say they prefer to remain with their current employer, even when they receive offers that pay more. | 如今三十岁以下员工中有很大一部分表示，即使收到薪水更高的录用通知，他们也愿意留在现在的雇主这里。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
