| **assume** | v. | ★假定,认为；承担；呈现 | Employers who assume that money is the only factor misunderstand what has changed. | 以为钱是唯一因素的雇主，误解了变化所在。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **conclude** | v. | ★得出结论；结束 | Workers still care about pay, but they also weigh the cost of constant uncertainty, and increasingly they conclude that the price is too high. | 员工仍然在意薪水，但他们也会衡量持续不确定性带来的代价，而且越来越多的人认为这个代价太高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **dramatically** | adv. | ★显著地；戏剧性地；引人注目地；剧烈地 | Weather forecasting has improved dramatically. | 天气预报的准确性已经大幅提高。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **ambitious** | adj. | ★有雄心的；野心勃勃的 | For decades, the standard advice given to ambitious young workers was to change jobs every few years, since moving was the fastest way to raise a salary. | 几十年来，给有抱负的年轻人的标准建议是每隔几年换一次工作，因为跳槽是涨薪最快的途径。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **circumstance** | n. | ★情况,环境；境遇 | In such circumstances, a familiar job where the rules are known feels less like a trap and more like a form of security. | 在这种情况下，一份规则清楚的熟悉工作，感觉就不像牢笼，而像一种安全感。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **compete** | v. | ★竞争,比赛 | Companies that wish to attract young talent would do better to offer predictable schedules and genuine opportunities to learn than to compete on salary alone. | 想吸引年轻人才的公司，与其只在薪资上竞争，不如提供可预期的作息和真正的学习机会。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **instrument** | n. | ★仪器,器械；乐器；工具 | A three-day forecast today is as accurate as a one-day forecast was thirty years ago, thanks to better instruments, more powerful computers and a deeper understanding of the atmosphere. | 借助更好的仪器、更强大的计算机和对大气更深入的认识，如今三天的预报已经和三十年前一天的预报一样准。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **interpret** | v. | ★解释,理解；口译 | When a forecast states that there is a thirty percent chance of rain, many listeners interpret this as a promise that it will not rain, and feel cheated if it does. | 当预报说有百分之三十的概率下雨时，许多听众把它理解成"不会下雨"的承诺，一旦下了雨就觉得被骗了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **simplify** | v. | ★简化,使简易 | Forecasters have been slow to explain probability in ordinary language, and television presenters often simplify the information until the uncertainty disappears. | 预报员一直不擅长用日常语言解释概率，电视主持人又常常把信息简化得连不确定性都消失了。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **education** | n. | ★教育；培养；教育学 | Although keyboards have replaced pens in most offices, researchers argue that handwriting still has a valuable role in education. | 尽管键盘在大多数办公室已经取代了笔，研究人员认为手写在教育中仍有宝贵的价值。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **write** | v. | ★写,书写；写作；写信 | The explanation seems to lie in the brain: writing by hand requires a sequence of precise movements, and this physical effort creates a stronger memory than simply choosing a letter from a list. | 解释似乎在大脑里：手写需要一连串精确的动作，这种身体上的付出比从列表里挑一个字母能形成更强的记忆。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **instead** | adv. | ★代替；反而,却 | It suggests instead that handwriting deserves a place alongside it, particularly in the early years when children are still learning to shape their thoughts. | 它只是提示，手写值得与技术并存，尤其是在孩子还在学习组织自己想法的早期阶段。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **young** | adj./n. | ★年轻的；幼小的；年轻人；幼崽 | Experiments with young children show that those who form letters by hand recognise them more quickly and remember them longer than children who only tap them on a screen. | 针对幼儿的实验显示，用手把字母写出来的孩子，比只在屏幕上点选的孩子认得更快、记得更牢。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **technology** | n. | ★技术,工艺；科技 | None of this means that technology should be banned from the classroom. | 这些都不意味着应该把技术赶出教室。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |

| **form** | v./n. | ★形成；构成；表格；形式 | Experiments with young children show that those who form letters by hand recognise them more quickly and remember them longer than children who only tap them on a screen. | 针对幼儿的实验显示，用手把字母写出来的孩子，比只在屏幕上点选的孩子认得更快、记得更牢。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **physical** | adj. | ★身体的；物质的；物理的 | The explanation seems to lie in the brain: writing by hand requires a sequence of precise movements, and this physical effort creates a stronger memory than simply choosing a letter from a list. | 解释似乎在大脑里：手写需要一连串精确的动作，这种身体上的付出比从列表里挑一个字母能形成更强的记忆。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **although** | conj. | ★虽然,尽管；然而 | Although keyboards have replaced pens in most offices, researchers argue that handwriting still has a valuable role in education. | 尽管键盘在大多数办公室已经取代了笔，研究人员认为手写在教育中仍有宝贵的价值。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |
| **difference** | n. | ★差别,差异；分歧 | Teachers who have observed the difference say that pupils also compose more carefully when they write slowly, because they cannot delete a sentence as easily as they can on a computer. | 观察到这种差别的教师还说，学生慢慢书写时构思也更用心，因为他们无法像在电脑上那样轻易删掉一个句子。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · The Value of Handwriting |

### 长篇阅读（34 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **local** | adj./n. | ★当地的；本地的；局部的；本地人 | Local governments have tried to regulate the industry, yet rules that apply to restaurants are difficult to enforce when the meal is assembled in a different district from the one where it is eaten. | 地方政府试图规范这个行业，然而当一顿饭是在与食用地不同的城区里装配出来时，适用于餐馆的规定很难执行。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **small** | adj. | ★小的；少量的；年幼的；微不足道的 | A single order typically arrives with a plastic container, a paper bag, a set of disposable chopsticks and several small packets of sauce, most of which are used for a few minutes and then thrown away. | 一份订单通常送到一个塑料盒、一个纸袋、一套一次性筷子和几小包调料，其中大部分用上几分钟就被扔掉。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **amount** | n./v. | ★数量,总额；总计 | The rapid growth of food delivery has created an enormous amount of packaging waste, and cities are struggling to deal with it. | 外卖行业的快速增长制造了数量惊人的包装垃圾，各城市正为此发愁。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **plastic** | n./adj. | ★塑料；塑料制品；塑胶的；可塑的；造作的 | A single order typically arrives with a plastic container, a paper bag, a set of disposable chopsticks and several small packets of sauce, most of which are used for a few minutes and then thrown away. | 一份订单通常送到一个塑料盒、一个纸袋、一套一次性筷子和几小包调料，其中大部分用上几分钟就被扔掉。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **bag** | n. | ★包；袋；提包 | A single order typically arrives with a plastic container, a paper bag, a set of disposable chopsticks and several small packets of sauce, most of which are used for a few minutes and then thrown away. | 一份订单通常送到一个塑料盒、一个纸袋、一套一次性筷子和几小包调料，其中大部分用上几分钟就被扔掉。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **deal** | v./n. | ★处理,应对；交易；大量 | The rapid growth of food delivery has created an enormous amount of packaging waste, and cities are struggling to deal with it. | 外卖行业的快速增长制造了数量惊人的包装垃圾，各城市正为此发愁。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **over** | prep./adv. | ★在…上方；超过；结束 | Environmental groups estimate that the volume of this waste has increased several times over in the past five years. | 环保团体估计，这类垃圾的总量在过去五年里翻了几番。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **rule** | n./v. | ★规则；统治；规定；裁定 | Local governments have tried to regulate the industry, yet rules that apply to restaurants are difficult to enforce when the meal is assembled in a different district from the one where it is eaten. | 地方政府试图规范这个行业，然而当一顿饭是在与食用地不同的城区里装配出来时，适用于餐馆的规定很难执行。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **think** | v. | ★认为,觉得；思考；想起 | Researchers suggest that the most effective measure would be a charge on single-use packaging, which would make customers think twice before ordering items they do not need. | 研究人员认为，最有效的办法是对一次性包装收费，这会让顾客在点多出来的东西之前三思。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |
| **waste** | n./v./adj. | ★浪费；废物,废料；荒地；消耗；浪费,滥用；使荒芜；废弃的；荒芜的 | The rapid growth of food delivery has created an enormous amount of packaging waste, and cities are struggling to deal with it. | 外卖行业的快速增长制造了数量惊人的包装垃圾，各城市正为此发愁。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · Food Delivery and Packaging Waste |

| **valuable** | adj./n. | ★有价值的,宝贵的；贵重的；贵重物品 | Others argue that experience gained outside the classroom is just as valuable as knowledge gained inside it. | 另一些人则认为，课堂之外获得的经验和课堂上学到的知识同样宝贵。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **view** | n./v. | ★视野；景色；观点,看法；视图；看待,考虑；观看；查看；视察 | In my view, a part-time job is beneficial provided that it does not interfere with study. | 在我看来，只要不耽误学习，兼职是有益的。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **colleague** | n. | ★同事 | Such work teaches students to manage their time, to communicate with colleagues and to take responsibility for tasks they cannot simply abandon. | 这类工作教会学生管理时间、与同事沟通，并对那些不能随手丢开的任务负责。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **provided** | conj. | ★假如,只要 | In my view, a part-time job is beneficial provided that it does not interfere with study. | 在我看来，只要不耽误学习，兼职是有益的。 | 2023年3月 第1套 · 范文 |

## 2022年12月


### 仔细阅读（167 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **they** | pron. | ★他们；她们；它们；人们 | Music is slower at weekends, when shoppers are less hurried and therefore more likely to buy things they had not planned. | 周末的音乐节奏更慢，因为那时购物者不赶时间，更容易买下原本没打算买的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **for** | prep./conj. | ★为了；因为；给；持续 | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **are** | v./aux. | ★是；存在(be 的复数) | Essentials such as milk, bread and eggs are deliberately placed at the back or on opposite sides of the store, so that customers must walk past hundreds of other products in order to reach them. | 牛奶、面包和鸡蛋这类必需品被有意放在商店最里面或相对的两侧，顾客为了拿到它们，必须经过几百种其他商品。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **who** | pron. | ★谁；…的人；他/她 | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **not** | adv. | ★不；没有；并非 | The route is not random. | 这条路线不是随机的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **have** | v./aux. | ★有；使；吃；已经(构成完成时) | Critics reply that the layout is designed to defeat self-control, and several countries have discussed regulations requiring stores to place healthy food in prominent positions. | 批评者则反驳说，这种布局就是设计来打败自制力的，一些国家已经在讨论立法，要求商店把健康食品放在显眼位置。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **has** | v./aux. | ★有(have 的第三人称单数) | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **than** | conj./prep. | ★比；与…相比 | Telemedicine expanded rapidly when patients could not visit clinics, and many of them discovered that a consultation conducted from home was more convenient than a journey across the city followed by an hour in a waiting room. | 当病人无法到诊所时，远程医疗迅速铺开，许多病人发现在家问诊，比横穿城市再在候诊室坐上一小时要方便得多。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **more** | adj./adv. | ★更多的；更；另外的 | Music is slower at weekends, when shoppers are less hurried and therefore more likely to buy things they had not planned. | 周末的音乐节奏更慢，因为那时购物者不赶时间，更容易买下原本没打算买的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **their** | pron. | ★他们的；她们的；它们的 | Doctors also report that some patients speak more frankly in their own living rooms than they do in a consulting room. | 医生还反映，有些病人在自家客厅里说得比在诊室里更坦率。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **people** | n. | ★人们；人民；民族；国民；家人 | Supermarkets defend these techniques as a service to customers, arguing that a logical arrangement helps people find what they need. | 超市为这些手法辩护，称这是对顾客的服务，认为符合逻辑的摆放有助于人们找到需要的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **time** | n. | ★时间；次,回；时代；时机；倍数 | For routine matters, such as renewing a prescription or discussing test results, the telephone or a video call is clearly sufficient, and it saves both the patient and the hospital a great deal of time. | 对于续方、讨论检查结果这类常规事务，电话或视频显然已经足够，而且为病人和医院都省下大量时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **rather** | adv. | ★相当；宁愿；更确切地说 | The reasonable conclusion is that telemedicine works best as an addition to the health service rather than a substitute for it, handling the simple cases so that the difficult ones receive the time they require. | 合理的结论是：远程医疗最好作为医疗服务的一种补充而非替代，由它处理简单的病例，好让复杂的病例获得所需的时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **should** | v./aux. | ★应该；应当；万一 | It is an argument, made of shelves, about what you should want. | 它是由货架搭成的一套论证，主题是"你应该想要什么"。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **work** | n./v. | ★工作；职业；作品；起作用；运转 | Whether such rules would work is uncertain, but the debate has made one thing clear: the modern supermarket is not a neutral space. | 这类规定是否有效尚不确定，但这场争论把一件事说清楚了：现代超市不是一个中立的空间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **child** | n. | ★儿童,小孩；子女；产物 | Items that generate the highest profit are positioned at eye level, while cheaper alternatives are placed on the lowest shelves, where adults rarely look and children do. | 利润最高的商品被摆在视线高度，而便宜的替代品被放在最低一层，成年人很少往那里看，孩子倒是看得到。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **cannot** | v./aux. | ★不能；无法 | A physician cannot examine an abdomen, feel a lump or observe how a patient walks down a corridor through a screen, and these physical signs are often what reveal a serious condition. | 医生无法隔着屏幕按压腹部、摸到肿块，或观察病人如何走过走廊，而这些体征往往正是发现重病的线索。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **difficult** | adj. | ★困难的；难对付的；难以取悦的 | The reasonable conclusion is that telemedicine works best as an addition to the health service rather than a substitute for it, handling the simple cases so that the difficult ones receive the time they require. | 合理的结论是：远程医疗最好作为医疗服务的一种补充而非替代，由它处理简单的病例，好让复杂的病例获得所需的时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **follow** | v. | ★跟随；遵循；听懂；结果是 | Telemedicine expanded rapidly when patients could not visit clinics, and many of them discovered that a consultation conducted from home was more convenient than a journey across the city followed by an hour in a waiting room. | 当病人无法到诊所时，远程医疗迅速铺开，许多病人发现在家问诊，比横穿城市再在候诊室坐上一小时要方便得多。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **health** | n. | ★健康；卫生；保健 | The reasonable conclusion is that telemedicine works best as an addition to the health service rather than a substitute for it, handling the simple cases so that the difficult ones receive the time they require. | 合理的结论是：远程医疗最好作为医疗服务的一种补充而非替代，由它处理简单的病例，好让复杂的病例获得所需的时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **argue** | v. | ★争论,主张 | Supermarkets defend these techniques as a service to customers, arguing that a logical arrangement helps people find what they need. | 超市为这些手法辩护，称这是对顾客的服务，认为符合逻辑的摆放有助于人们找到需要的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **made** | v. | ★制作；使得(make 的过去式/过去分词) | Whether such rules would work is uncertain, but the debate has made one thing clear: the modern supermarket is not a neutral space. | 这类规定是否有效尚不确定，但这场争论把一件事说清楚了：现代超市不是一个中立的空间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **question** | n./v. | ★问题；疑问；质疑；询问 | There is also the question of who is excluded: elderly patients without a smartphone, and those whose hearing or sight makes a screen impractical, are precisely the people who need medical attention most. | 还有一个"谁被排除在外"的问题：没有智能手机的老年患者，以及视力听力让他们用不了屏幕的人，恰恰是最需要医疗关注的群体。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **rarely** | adv. | ★很少,难得；不常；异乎寻常地；极好地 | Items that generate the highest profit are positioned at eye level, while cheaper alternatives are placed on the lowest shelves, where adults rarely look and children do. | 利润最高的商品被摆在视线高度，而便宜的替代品被放在最低一层，成年人很少往那里看，孩子倒是看得到。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **yet** | adv./conj. | ★然而；还；但是 | Yet there are limits that no improvement in video quality will remove. | 然而有些局限是视频画质再怎么改善也消除不了的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **leave** | v./n. | ★离开；留下；休假 | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **patient** | n./adj. | ★病人；有耐心的 | Telemedicine expanded rapidly when patients could not visit clinics, and many of them discovered that a consultation conducted from home was more convenient than a journey across the city followed by an hour in a waiting room. | 当病人无法到诊所时，远程医疗迅速铺开，许多病人发现在家问诊，比横穿城市再在候诊室坐上一小时要方便得多。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **result** | n./v. | ★结果,成果；成绩；导致；起因于 | For routine matters, such as renewing a prescription or discussing test results, the telephone or a video call is clearly sufficient, and it saves both the patient and the hospital a great deal of time. | 对于续方、讨论检查结果这类常规事务，电话或视频显然已经足够，而且为病人和医院都省下大量时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **customer** | n. | ★顾客,客户 | Essentials such as milk, bread and eggs are deliberately placed at the back or on opposite sides of the store, so that customers must walk past hundreds of other products in order to reach them. | 牛奶、面包和鸡蛋这类必需品被有意放在商店最里面或相对的两侧，顾客为了拿到它们，必须经过几百种其他商品。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **likely** | adj./adv. | ★可能的；很可能 | Music is slower at weekends, when shoppers are less hurried and therefore more likely to buy things they had not planned. | 周末的音乐节奏更慢，因为那时购物者不赶时间，更容易买下原本没打算买的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **country** | n. | ★国家；乡村；地区 | Critics reply that the layout is designed to defeat self-control, and several countries have discussed regulations requiring stores to place healthy food in prominent positions. | 批评者则反驳说，这种布局就是设计来打败自制力的，一些国家已经在讨论立法，要求商店把健康食品放在显眼位置。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **critics** | n. | ★批评家；评论家(critic 的复数) | Critics reply that the layout is designed to defeat self-control, and several countries have discussed regulations requiring stores to place healthy food in prominent positions. | 批评者则反驳说，这种布局就是设计来打败自制力的，一些国家已经在讨论立法，要求商店把健康食品放在显眼位置。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **goods** | n. | ★商品；货物 | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **item** | n. | ★项目,条款；一件(商品) | Items that generate the highest profit are positioned at eye level, while cheaper alternatives are placed on the lowest shelves, where adults rarely look and children do. | 利润最高的商品被摆在视线高度，而便宜的替代品被放在最低一层，成年人很少往那里看，孩子倒是看得到。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **journey** | n. | ★旅程；行程；旅行 | Telemedicine expanded rapidly when patients could not visit clinics, and many of them discovered that a consultation conducted from home was more convenient than a journey across the city followed by an hour in a waiting room. | 当病人无法到诊所时，远程医疗迅速铺开，许多病人发现在家问诊，比横穿城市再在候诊室坐上一小时要方便得多。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **look** | v./n. | ★看；看起来；神情 | Items that generate the highest profit are positioned at eye level, while cheaper alternatives are placed on the lowest shelves, where adults rarely look and children do. | 利润最高的商品被摆在视线高度，而便宜的替代品被放在最低一层，成年人很少往那里看，孩子倒是看得到。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **music** | n. | ★音乐；乐曲；乐谱 | Music is slower at weekends, when shoppers are less hurried and therefore more likely to buy things they had not planned. | 周末的音乐节奏更慢，因为那时购物者不赶时间，更容易买下原本没打算买的东西。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **quality** | n./adj. | ★质量,品质；性质；优质的 | Yet there are limits that no improvement in video quality will remove. | 然而有些局限是视频画质再怎么改善也消除不了的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **rapid** | adj. | ★迅速的,快的 | Telemedicine expanded rapidly when patients could not visit clinics, and many of them discovered that a consultation conducted from home was more convenient than a journey across the city followed by an hour in a waiting room. | 当病人无法到诊所时，远程医疗迅速铺开，许多病人发现在家问诊，比横穿城市再在候诊室坐上一小时要方便得多。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **route** | n. | ★路线；途径；航线 | The route is not random. | 这条路线不是随机的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **screen** | n./v. | ★屏幕；屏风；帘；筛子；掩蔽；放映；遮蔽；筛查；甄别 | A physician cannot examine an abdomen, feel a lump or observe how a patient walks down a corridor through a screen, and these physical signs are often what reveal a serious condition. | 医生无法隔着屏幕按压腹部、摸到肿块，或观察病人如何走过走廊，而这些体征往往正是发现重病的线索。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **simple** | adj. | ★简单的；朴素的；单纯的 | The reasonable conclusion is that telemedicine works best as an addition to the health service rather than a substitute for it, handling the simple cases so that the difficult ones receive the time they require. | 合理的结论是：远程医疗最好作为医疗服务的一种补充而非替代，由它处理简单的病例，好让复杂的病例获得所需的时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **routine** | n./adj. | ★例行公事；常规；常规的；日常的 | For routine matters, such as renewing a prescription or discussing test results, the telephone or a video call is clearly sufficient, and it saves both the patient and the hospital a great deal of time. | 对于续方、讨论检查结果这类常规事务，电话或视频显然已经足够，而且为病人和医院都省下大量时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **essential** | adj./n. | ★必不可少的；本质的；要素 | Essentials such as milk, bread and eggs are deliberately placed at the back or on opposite sides of the store, so that customers must walk past hundreds of other products in order to reach them. | 牛奶、面包和鸡蛋这类必需品被有意放在商店最里面或相对的两侧，顾客为了拿到它们，必须经过几百种其他商品。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **random** | adj. | ★随机的,任意的；胡乱的 | The route is not random. | 这条路线不是随机的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **layout** | n. | ★布局,安排；版面设计 | Anyone who has entered a supermarket for a litre of milk and left with a basket full of goods has experienced the most carefully studied layout in retail. | 任何一位为了一升牛奶走进超市、最后拎着满满一篮子商品出来的人，都体验过零售业里研究得最透彻的布局。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **prominent** | adj. | ★突出的,显著的；著名的 | Critics reply that the layout is designed to defeat self-control, and several countries have discussed regulations requiring stores to place healthy food in prominent positions. | 批评者则反驳说，这种布局就是设计来打败自制力的，一些国家已经在讨论立法，要求商店把健康食品放在显眼位置。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **had** | v./aux. | ★有(have 的过去式)；构成过去完成时 | During the pandemic, public transport was regarded as risky, and millions of people who had never considered cycling bought a bicycle instead. | 疫情期间，公共交通被视为有风险，数以百万计从未考虑过骑车的人转而买了自行车。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · The Return of the Bicycle |

### 长篇阅读（28 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **school** | n. | ★学校；学院；上学期间；学派 | When schools closed, online education was praised as the solution that would keep learning alive. | 学校停课时，在线教育被誉为能让学习延续下去的解决方案。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **well** | adv./adj./int./n./v. | ★好,令人满意地；完全地；详细地；健康的；良好的；井；源泉；涌出 | Recorded lectures work reasonably well for students who are disciplined and have a quiet place to study, but they fail precisely where schools matter most: with children who need encouragement, structure and someone to notice when they have stopped understanding. | 录播课对自律、又有安静地方学习的学生还算管用，但它恰恰在学校最关键的地方失灵了：那些需要鼓励、需要有人管束、需要有人发现他们已经听不懂的孩子。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **praise** | n./v. | ★赞扬,表扬；赞美 | When schools closed, online education was praised as the solution that would keep learning alive. | 学校停课时，在线教育被誉为能让学习延续下去的解决方案。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **replace** | v. | ★取代,替换；更换；把…放回原处 | The pandemic did not replace the classroom; it revealed what the classroom was for. | 疫情并没有取代课堂，它只是暴露了课堂是用来干什么的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **classroom** | n. | ★教室；课堂 | The pandemic did not replace the classroom; it revealed what the classroom was for. | 疫情并没有取代课堂，它只是暴露了课堂是用来干什么的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **keep** | v. | ★保持,继续；保存；饲养 | When schools closed, online education was praised as the solution that would keep learning alive. | 学校停课时，在线教育被誉为能让学习延续下去的解决方案。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **reveal** | v. | ★揭示,透露；显露,展现 | The pandemic did not replace the classroom; it revealed what the classroom was for. | 疫情并没有取代课堂，它只是暴露了课堂是用来干什么的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **did** | v. | ★做(do 的过去式)；助动词 | The pandemic did not replace the classroom; it revealed what the classroom was for. | 疫情并没有取代课堂，它只是暴露了课堂是用来干什么的。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **hand** | n./v. | ★手；指针；人手；递给 | On the other hand, digital material has proved genuinely useful for revision, and many students say they value the ability to replay a difficult explanation. | 另一方面，数字资料在复习上确实有用，许多学生说他们看重能够重播难懂的讲解这一点。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |
| **material** | n./adj. | ★材料,原料；资料；物质的 | On the other hand, digital material has proved genuinely useful for revision, and many students say they value the ability to replay a difficult explanation. | 另一方面，数字资料在复习上确实有用，许多学生说他们看重能够重播难懂的讲解这一点。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |

| **insist** | v. | ★坚持,坚决主张 | Most institutions have now settled on a mixture, keeping online resources while insisting that the core of teaching happens in person. | 大多数院校现在选择了混合方式：保留线上资源，同时坚持教学的核心仍在线下发生。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |

| **notice** | v./n. | ★注意到；通知；注意；布告 | Recorded lectures work reasonably well for students who are disciplined and have a quiet place to study, but they fail precisely where schools matter most: with children who need encouragement, structure and someone to notice when they have stopped understanding. | 录播课对自律、又有安静地方学习的学生还算管用，但它恰恰在学校最关键的地方失灵了：那些需要鼓励、需要有人管束、需要有人发现他们已经听不懂的孩子。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Online Education after the Pandemic |

### 听力（45 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **patient** | n./adj. | ★病人；有耐心的 | Some clinics have begun to prescribe exercise alongside medication, and early reports suggest that patients who follow such advice recover slightly faster. | 一些诊所已经开始在开药的同时"开"运动，早期报告显示，遵从这类建议的患者康复得略快一些。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **thousand** | num./n. | ★一千；成千上万 | A study that followed more than ten thousand adults for fifteen years has found that those who exercised regularly were significantly less likely to develop depression. | 一项对一万多名成年人跟踪了十五年的研究发现，经常锻炼的人患抑郁症的概率明显更低。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **whether** | conj. | ★是否；不管,无论 | Young couples interviewed by reporters said they welcomed the payment but were more concerned about whether they could find affordable childcare near their workplace. | 接受记者采访的年轻夫妇表示，他们欢迎补贴，但更关心工作地点附近能否找到负担得起的托儿服务。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **among** | prep. | ★在…之中；在…之间 | The effect appeared even among people who did nothing more than walk for half an hour a day, which suggests that the intensity of the activity matters less than its regularity. | 即使是在那些每天不过散步半小时的人身上，这种效果也出现了，这说明运动的强度不如规律性重要。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **develop** | v. | ★发展；培养；开发；冲洗(照片) | A study that followed more than ten thousand adults for fifteen years has found that those who exercised regularly were significantly less likely to develop depression. | 一项对一万多名成年人跟踪了十五年的研究发现，经常锻炼的人患抑郁症的概率明显更低。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **month** | n. | ★月,月份；一个月时间 | The local government has announced a package of measures intended to encourage couples to have children, including a monthly payment for each child under three and a longer period of paid leave for both parents. | 当地政府宣布了一揽子鼓励夫妻生育的措施，其中包括为每个三岁以下儿童发放月度补贴，以及延长父母双方的带薪假期。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **near** | prep./adj. | ★靠近；接近的 | Young couples interviewed by reporters said they welcomed the payment but were more concerned about whether they could find affordable childcare near their workplace. | 接受记者采访的年轻夫妇表示，他们欢迎补贴，但更关心工作地点附近能否找到负担得起的托儿服务。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **said** | v. | ★说；讲(say 的过去式/过去分词) | Young couples interviewed by reporters said they welcomed the payment but were more concerned about whether they could find affordable childcare near their workplace. | 接受记者采访的年轻夫妇表示，他们欢迎补贴，但更关心工作地点附近能否找到负担得起的托儿服务。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **emphasise** | v. | ★强调；着重；使突出 | The authors of the study emphasise that exercise should not be presented as a replacement for professional treatment, but as a habit that supports it. | 研究作者强调，运动不应被宣传成专业治疗的替代品，而应被当作一种支持治疗的日常习惯。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **regular** | adj. | ★定期的,有规律的；常规的；正规的 | A study that followed more than ten thousand adults for fifteen years has found that those who exercised regularly were significantly less likely to develop depression. | 一项对一万多名成年人跟踪了十五年的研究发现，经常锻炼的人患抑郁症的概率明显更低。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **cause** | n./v. | ★原因；理由；事业；引起；导致 | Researchers were careful to note that the relationship is not simply one of cause and effect: people who are already depressed tend to stop exercising, so part of the difference may reflect the illness rather than the cure. | 研究人员谨慎地指出，这并不只是简单的因果关系：已经抑郁的人往往会停止锻炼，所以部分差异也许反映的是疾病本身，而不是疗效。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **payment** | n. | ★支付；付款；报酬 | The local government has announced a package of measures intended to encourage couples to have children, including a monthly payment for each child under three and a longer period of paid leave for both parents. | 当地政府宣布了一揽子鼓励夫妻生育的措施，其中包括为每个三岁以下儿童发放月度补贴，以及延长父母双方的带薪假期。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **present** | n./adj./v. | ★礼物；目前；出席的；现在的；呈现；提出；颁发 | The authors of the study emphasise that exercise should not be presented as a replacement for professional treatment, but as a habit that supports it. | 研究作者强调，运动不应被宣传成专业治疗的替代品，而应被当作一种支持治疗的日常习惯。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **relationship** | n. | ★关系；关联；亲属关系 | Researchers were careful to note that the relationship is not simply one of cause and effect: people who are already depressed tend to stop exercising, so part of the difference may reflect the illness rather than the cure. | 研究人员谨慎地指出，这并不只是简单的因果关系：已经抑郁的人往往会停止锻炼，所以部分差异也许反映的是疾病本身，而不是疗效。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **response** | n. | ★回答；反应,响应；回应 | Officials say the policy is a response to a sharp decline in the birth rate over the past decade, which has already forced several primary schools in rural districts to close. | 官员说，这项政策是对过去十年出生率急剧下降的回应，出生率下降已经迫使农村地区的几所小学关闭。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **advice** | n. | ★建议；忠告；意见 | Some clinics have begun to prescribe exercise alongside medication, and early reports suggest that patients who follow such advice recover slightly faster. | 一些诊所已经开始在开药的同时"开"运动，早期报告显示，遵从这类建议的患者康复得略快一些。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **announce** | v. | ★宣布,宣告 | The local government has announced a package of measures intended to encourage couples to have children, including a monthly payment for each child under three and a longer period of paid leave for both parents. | 当地政府宣布了一揽子鼓励夫妻生育的措施，其中包括为每个三岁以下儿童发放月度补贴，以及延长父母双方的带薪假期。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **concern** | n./v. | ★关心,担忧；涉及；事项 | Young couples interviewed by reporters said they welcomed the payment but were more concerned about whether they could find affordable childcare near their workplace. | 接受记者采访的年轻夫妇表示，他们欢迎补贴，但更关心工作地点附近能否找到负担得起的托儿服务。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **control** | n./v. | ★控制；支配 | Nevertheless, the association remained strong after the team had controlled for income, education and physical health. | 不过，在控制了收入、教育程度和身体健康状况之后，这种关联依然很强。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **economists** | n. | ★经济学家(economist 的复数) | Economists point out that financial support alone rarely changes such decisions, because the cost of raising a child involves far more than money: it includes housing, education and, above all, time. | 经济学家指出，单靠资金支持很少能改变这类决定，因为养育一个孩子的成本远不止钱：它还包括住房、教育，尤其是时间。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **primary** | adj. | ★主要的,首要的；初级的；基本的 | Officials say the policy is a response to a sharp decline in the birth rate over the past decade, which has already forced several primary schools in rural districts to close. | 官员说，这项政策是对过去十年出生率急剧下降的回应，出生率下降已经迫使农村地区的几所小学关闭。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **treatment** | n. | ★治疗,疗法；处理；对待,待遇；论述 | The authors of the study emphasise that exercise should not be presented as a replacement for professional treatment, but as a habit that supports it. | 研究作者强调，运动不应被宣传成专业治疗的替代品，而应被当作一种支持治疗的日常习惯。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **programme** | n. | ★节目；计划；方案 | The government has promised to build more nurseries, though it has not yet specified how the programme will be financed. | 政府承诺会建更多托儿所，但尚未说明这个项目将如何筹资。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **decline** | n./v. | ★下降,衰退；婉拒 | Officials say the policy is a response to a sharp decline in the birth rate over the past decade, which has already forced several primary schools in rural districts to close. | 官员说，这项政策是对过去十年出生率急剧下降的回应，出生率下降已经迫使农村地区的几所小学关闭。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **affordable** | adj. | ★负担得起的,价格合理的 | Young couples interviewed by reporters said they welcomed the payment but were more concerned about whether they could find affordable childcare near their workplace. | 接受记者采访的年轻夫妇表示，他们欢迎补贴，但更关心工作地点附近能否找到负担得起的托儿服务。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **finance** | n./v. | ★财政,金融；资金；资助 | The government has promised to build more nurseries, though it has not yet specified how the programme will be financed. | 政府承诺会建更多托儿所，但尚未说明这个项目将如何筹资。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **nevertheless** | adv. | ★然而,不过 | Nevertheless, the association remained strong after the team had controlled for income, education and physical health. | 不过，在控制了收入、教育程度和身体健康状况之后，这种关联依然很强。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **rural** | adj. | ★农村的,乡村的 | Officials say the policy is a response to a sharp decline in the birth rate over the past decade, which has already forced several primary schools in rural districts to close. | 官员说，这项政策是对过去十年出生率急剧下降的回应，出生率下降已经迫使农村地区的几所小学关闭。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **depress** | v. | ★使沮丧；使萧条 | Researchers were careful to note that the relationship is not simply one of cause and effect: people who are already depressed tend to stop exercising, so part of the difference may reflect the illness rather than the cure. | 研究人员谨慎地指出，这并不只是简单的因果关系：已经抑郁的人往往会停止锻炼，所以部分差异也许反映的是疾病本身，而不是疗效。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **specify** | v. | ★明确说明,具体指定 | The government has promised to build more nurseries, though it has not yet specified how the programme will be financed. | 政府承诺会建更多托儿所，但尚未说明这个项目将如何筹资。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **prescribe** | v. | ★开(药方)；规定,指示 | Some clinics have begun to prescribe exercise alongside medication, and early reports suggest that patients who follow such advice recover slightly faster. | 一些诊所已经开始在开药的同时"开"运动，早期报告显示，遵从这类建议的患者康复得略快一些。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **great** | adj. | ★伟大的；巨大的；极好的 | Ancient China made remarkable contributions to world civilisation, and the four great inventions are the best known among them: papermaking, printing, gunpowder and the compass. | 古代中国对世界文明作出了卓越贡献，其中最广为人知的是四大发明：造纸术、印刷术、火药和指南针。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · 四大发明 |
| **china** | n. | ★中国；瓷器 | Ancient China made remarkable contributions to world civilisation, and the four great inventions are the best known among them: papermaking, printing, gunpowder and the compass. | 古代中国对世界文明作出了卓越贡献，其中最广为人知的是四大发明：造纸术、印刷术、火药和指南针。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · 四大发明 |

| **knowledge** | n. | ★知识；学问；了解；认识 | Papermaking and printing made it possible to record and spread knowledge at a much lower cost, which promoted the development of education. | 造纸术和印刷术使知识的记录与传播成本大大降低，推动了教育的发展。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · 四大发明 |

| **sleep** | v./n. | ★睡觉；睡眠 | First, it helps to keep a regular schedule, because irregular sleep quickly weakens both concentration and mood. | 首先，保持规律的作息有帮助，因为作息紊乱会很快削弱注意力和情绪。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · 范文 |

| **mental** | adj. | ★心理的,精神的；智力的 | Mental health has become a topic that universities can no longer ignore, and I believe students themselves can do a great deal to protect it. | 心理健康已经成为大学无法再回避的话题，我认为学生自己可以做很多事来保护它。 | 2022年12月 第1套 · 范文 |

## 2022年6月


### 仔细阅读（135 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **than** | conj./prep. | ★比；与…相比 | Yet a series of experiments suggests that the wrong kind of praise can make children more fragile rather than more confident. | 然而一系列实验表明，错误的表扬方式会让孩子更脆弱，而不是更自信。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **student** | n. | ★学生；大学生；研究者 | Teachers who have adopted this approach report that students become more willing to attempt questions they cannot immediately answer, and that classrooms become quieter places where mistakes are discussed rather than hidden. | 采用这种做法的教师反映，学生更愿意去尝试不能立刻答出的问题，课堂也变得更安静，错误被拿出来讨论，而不是被藏起来。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **them** | pron. | ★他们；她们；它们 | Where buses are frequent, predictable and clean, people use them regardless of income; where services are irregular, even passengers who cannot afford a car will borrow money to avoid them. | 在公交班次密集、准点、干净的地方，无论收入高低人们都会坐；在服务不稳定的地方，连买不起车的人也会借钱躲开公交。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **can** | v./aux. | ★能；可以；可能 | Yet a series of experiments suggests that the wrong kind of praise can make children more fragile rather than more confident. | 然而一系列实验表明，错误的表扬方式会让孩子更脆弱，而不是更自信。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **this** | adj./pron. | ★这；这个；这样 | Teachers who have adopted this approach report that students become more willing to attempt questions they cannot immediately answer, and that classrooms become quieter places where mistakes are discussed rather than hidden. | 采用这种做法的教师反映，学生更愿意去尝试不能立刻答出的问题，课堂也变得更安静，错误被拿出来讨论，而不是被藏起来。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **time** | n. | ★时间；次,回；时代；时机；倍数 | Reliability is the decisive factor, because a journey that may take twenty minutes or fifty cannot be planned around, and an unplannable journey is useless for anyone who must arrive at a fixed time. | 可靠性是决定性因素，因为一段可能二十分钟也可能五十分钟的旅程无法用来安排事情，而凡是必须按时到达的人，都用不上一趟无法计划的行程。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **were** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去式复数) | In one study, pupils who were told they were clever after solving a simple puzzle later chose easier tasks, apparently because they wished to protect the reputation they had just been given. | 在一项研究中，因解出一道简单谜题而被夸"聪明"的学生，后来选择了更简单的任务，显然是想保护刚刚获得的那个名声。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **become** | v. | ★变成,成为；变得；适合 | Pupils who were praised for the method they used, or for the effort they had made, behaved in the opposite way: they selected harder problems and persisted longer when the work became difficult. | 而因所用方法或所付出努力受到表扬的学生，表现恰恰相反：他们选更难的题目，并且在变难时坚持得更久。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **city** | n. | ★城市,都市；全市居民 | Cities invest heavily in public transport and then wonder why residents continue to drive. | 城市在公共交通上投入巨资，随后又纳闷居民为什么还在开车。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **because** | conj. | ★因为；由于 | In one study, pupils who were told they were clever after solving a simple puzzle later chose easier tasks, apparently because they wished to protect the reputation they had just been given. | 在一项研究中，因解出一道简单谜题而被夸"聪明"的学生，后来选择了更简单的任务，显然是想保护刚刚获得的那个名声。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **rather** | adv. | ★相当；宁愿；更确切地说 | Yet a series of experiments suggests that the wrong kind of praise can make children more fragile rather than more confident. | 然而一系列实验表明，错误的表扬方式会让孩子更脆弱，而不是更自信。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **work** | n./v. | ★工作；职业；作品；起作用；运转 | Pupils who were praised for the method they used, or for the effort they had made, behaved in the opposite way: they selected harder problems and persisted longer when the work became difficult. | 而因所用方法或所付出努力受到表扬的学生，表现恰恰相反：他们选更难的题目，并且在变难时坚持得更久。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **study** | n./v. | ★学习；研究；书房；仔细察看 | In one study, pupils who were told they were clever after solving a simple puzzle later chose easier tasks, apparently because they wished to protect the reputation they had just been given. | 在一项研究中，因解出一道简单谜题而被夸"聪明"的学生，后来选择了更简单的任务，显然是想保护刚刚获得的那个名声。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **had** | v./aux. | ★有(have 的过去式)；构成过去完成时 | In one study, pupils who were told they were clever after solving a simple puzzle later chose easier tasks, apparently because they wished to protect the reputation they had just been given. | 在一项研究中，因解出一道简单谜题而被夸"聪明"的学生，后来选择了更简单的任务，显然是想保护刚刚获得的那个名声。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **will** | v./aux. | ★将；会；愿意 | Where buses are frequent, predictable and clean, people use them regardless of income; where services are irregular, even passengers who cannot afford a car will borrow money to avoid them. | 在公交班次密集、准点、干净的地方，无论收入高低人们都会坐；在服务不稳定的地方，连买不起车的人也会借钱躲开公交。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **change** | n./v. | ★变化；零钱；改变；更换 | Convenience follows closely behind: a route that requires two changes and a long walk is rejected even by commuters who describe themselves as environmentally conscious. | 紧随其后的是便利性：一条要换乘两次再走很长一段路的线路，连自称有环保意识的上班族都会拒绝。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **may** | v./aux. | ★可以；可能；也许 | Reliability is the decisive factor, because a journey that may take twenty minutes or fifty cannot be planned around, and an unplannable journey is useless for anyone who must arrive at a fixed time. | 可靠性是决定性因素，因为一段可能二十分钟也可能五十分钟的旅程无法用来安排事情，而凡是必须按时到达的人，都用不上一趟无法计划的行程。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **other** | adj./pron. | ★其他的；另外的；另一个 | The car, in other words, is not defeated by argument. | 换句话说，汽车不是被道理打败的。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **use** | v./n. | ★使用；利用；用途 | Pupils who were praised for the method they used, or for the effort they had made, behaved in the opposite way: they selected harder problems and persisted longer when the work became difficult. | 而因所用方法或所付出努力受到表扬的学生，表现恰恰相反：他们选更难的题目，并且在变难时坚持得更久。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **what** | pron./adj. | ★什么；…的；多么 | The difference lies in what the child learns to believe. | 差别在于孩子由此学会了相信什么。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **child** | n. | ★儿童,小孩；子女；产物 | Parents and teachers are constantly advised to praise children, and the advice is seldom questioned. | 家长和老师不断被建议要多表扬孩子，而这条建议很少被质疑。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **learn** | v. | ★学习；得知；记住 | The difference lies in what the child learns to believe. | 差别在于孩子由此学会了相信什么。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **make** | v. | ★使得,让；做,制造；成为；构成 | Yet a series of experiments suggests that the wrong kind of praise can make children more fragile rather than more confident. | 然而一系列实验表明，错误的表扬方式会让孩子更脆弱，而不是更自信。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **report** | n./v. | ★报告,报道；汇报；举报；传闻 | Teachers who have adopted this approach report that students become more willing to attempt questions they cannot immediately answer, and that classrooms become quieter places where mistakes are discussed rather than hidden. | 采用这种做法的教师反映，学生更愿意去尝试不能立刻答出的问题，课堂也变得更安静，错误被拿出来讨论，而不是被藏起来。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **require** | v. | ★需要；要求,规定 | Convenience follows closely behind: a route that requires two changes and a long walk is rejected even by commuters who describe themselves as environmentally conscious. | 紧随其后的是便利性：一条要换乘两次再走很长一段路的线路，连自称有环保意识的上班族都会拒绝。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **suggest** | v. | ★建议,提议；暗示；表明；使想起 | Yet a series of experiments suggests that the wrong kind of praise can make children more fragile rather than more confident. | 然而一系列实验表明，错误的表扬方式会让孩子更脆弱，而不是更自信。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **problem** | n. | ★问题,难题；习题 | Pupils who were praised for the method they used, or for the effort they had made, behaved in the opposite way: they selected harder problems and persisted longer when the work became difficult. | 而因所用方法或所付出努力受到表扬的学生，表现恰恰相反：他们选更难的题目，并且在变难时坚持得更久。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **teacher** | n. | ★教师,老师；导师 | Parents and teachers are constantly advised to praise children, and the advice is seldom questioned. | 家长和老师不断被建议要多表扬孩子，而这条建议很少被质疑。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **been** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去分词) | In one study, pupils who were told they were clever after solving a simple puzzle later chose easier tasks, apparently because they wished to protect the reputation they had just been given. | 在一项研究中，因解出一道简单谜题而被夸"聪明"的学生，后来选择了更简单的任务，显然是想保护刚刚获得的那个名声。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **where** | adv./conj. | ★在哪里；在…的地方 | Teachers who have adopted this approach report that students become more willing to attempt questions they cannot immediately answer, and that classrooms become quieter places where mistakes are discussed rather than hidden. | 采用这种做法的教师反映，学生更愿意去尝试不能立刻答出的问题，课堂也变得更安静，错误被拿出来讨论，而不是被藏起来。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **every** | adj. | ★每一个；所有的；每隔 | Doubling the number of buses on a route costs more than a poster, but it changes the calculation that every passenger makes before leaving the house. | 把某条线路的公交车数量翻倍，比贴一张海报贵，但它改变了每位乘客出门前心里的那笔账。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **take** | v. | ★拿,取；花费(时间)；乘坐；接受 | Reliability is the decisive factor, because a journey that may take twenty minutes or fifty cannot be planned around, and an unplannable journey is useless for anyone who must arrive at a fixed time. | 可靠性是决定性因素，因为一段可能二十分钟也可能五十分钟的旅程无法用来安排事情，而凡是必须按时到达的人，都用不上一趟无法计划的行程。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **twenty** | num. | ★二十 | Fortunately, most vehicles stand unused for more than twenty hours a day, which means the time of charging can be shifted without much inconvenience. | 好在多数车辆一天里有二十多个小时是停着不用的，这意味着充电时间可以挪动，并不会带来太多不便。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |
| **vehicle** | n. | ★车辆,交通工具；媒介,工具；传播媒介；载体 | As electric vehicles become common, engineers have begun to worry about the network that supplies the electricity. | 随着电动汽车普及，工程师开始担心供电网络。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |
| **able** | adj. | ★能够的；有能力的 | Some researchers go further and propose that cars should be able to sell electricity back to the grid during periods of high demand. | 一些研究者更进一步，提出汽车应该能在用电高峰时把电卖回给电网。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |
| **supply** | n./v. | ★供应,供给；补给；供应量；提供 | As electric vehicles become common, engineers have begun to worry about the network that supplies the electricity. | 随着电动汽车普及，工程师开始担心供电网络。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |
| **household** | n./adj. | ★家庭,户；家用的 | If millions of drivers charge their cars in the evening, when demand from households is already at its highest, the system could be overloaded in particular districts. | 如果数百万车主都在傍晚充电，而那时家庭用电本来就处于高峰，某些区域的电网可能会过载。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |
| **propose** | v. | ★提议,建议；打算；求婚 | Some researchers go further and propose that cars should be able to sell electricity back to the grid during periods of high demand. | 一些研究者更进一步，提出汽车应该能在用电高峰时把电卖回给电网。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · Electric Cars and the Power Grid |

| **exchange** | n./v. | ★交换,交流；兑换 | Whether the exchange is worthwhile depends on what stage of life a person has reached. | 这笔交换划不划算，取决于一个人处在人生的哪个阶段。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **lived** | v. | ★居住(live 的过去式) | Housing consumes a large share of income, the daily commute exhausts people before they begin work, and neighbours who have lived in the same building for years may never learn each other's names. | 住房占去收入的很大一部分，每天通勤让人还没开始工作就已经疲惫，住在同一栋楼里几年的邻居可能连彼此的名字都不知道。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **living** | n./adj. | ★生活；生计；活着的 | Living in a big city offers opportunities that small towns cannot match. | 住在大城市有小城镇给不了的机会。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **opportunity** | n. | ★机会,时机 | Living in a big city offers opportunities that small towns cannot match. | 住在大城市有小城镇给不了的机会。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **quiet** | adj./n. | ★安静的；平静的；安静 | For someone who values space, quiet and a slower pace, it is not. | 对看重空间、安静和慢节奏的人来说，则不是。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **someone** | pron. | ★某人；有人 | For someone eager to learn and to meet people from different backgrounds, the city is probably the better choice. | 对渴望学习、想结识不同背景的人来说，城市大概是更好的选择。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **training** | n. | ★训练；培训；培养 | Hospitals, universities and theatres are within reach, and a graduate is far more likely to find a job that suits their training. | 医院、大学和剧院近在眼前，毕业生也更可能找到与所学对口的工作。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **obvious** | adj. | ★明显的,显而易见的 | At the same time, city life has obvious drawbacks. | 与此同时，城市生活的弊端也很明显。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **pace** | n./v. | ★速度,节奏；步速；踱步 | For someone who values space, quiet and a slower pace, it is not. | 对看重空间、安静和慢节奏的人来说，则不是。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **stage** | n. | ★阶段；舞台；时期 | Whether the exchange is worthwhile depends on what stage of life a person has reached. | 这笔交换划不划算，取决于一个人处在人生的哪个阶段。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **eager** | adj. | ★渴望的,热切的 | For someone eager to learn and to meet people from different backgrounds, the city is probably the better choice. | 对渴望学习、想结识不同背景的人来说，城市大概是更好的选择。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **worthwhile** | adj. | ★值得做的,值得花时间的；有价值的；有用的；合算的；值得的；有益的；值得花时间的；值得努力的 | Whether the exchange is worthwhile depends on what stage of life a person has reached. | 这笔交换划不划算，取决于一个人处在人生的哪个阶段。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **commute** | v./n. | ★通勤,上下班往返；通勤路程；减刑；交换；通勤路程 | Housing consumes a large share of income, the daily commute exhausts people before they begin work, and neighbours who have lived in the same building for years may never learn each other's names. | 住房占去收入的很大一部分，每天通勤让人还没开始工作就已经疲惫，住在同一栋楼里几年的邻居可能连彼此的名字都不知道。 | 2022年6月 第1套 · 范文 |

## 2021年12月


### 仔细阅读（129 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **they** | pron. | ★他们；她们；它们；人们 | Shoppers who would ignore an item at its original price suddenly want it, not because they need it but because they feel they would be foolish to miss the opportunity. | 按原价时根本不会看一眼的购物者，突然就想要了，不是因为需要，而是因为觉得错过这个机会太蠢。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **for** | prep./conj. | ★为了；因为；给；持续 | Nature reserves are usually drawn on maps as if the land were empty, but in most cases people have lived on it for generations. | 自然保护区在地图上画出来时，那片土地仿佛是空的，但在多数情况下，人们已经在那里世代居住。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **are** | v./aux. | ★是；存在(be 的复数) | Experiments show that customers are more likely to buy when they can see the comparison, even when the so-called original price was never actually charged. | 实验表明，只要能看到对照，顾客就更可能下单，哪怕那个所谓的原价其实从未真正收过。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **who** | pron. | ★谁；…的人；他/她 | Shoppers who would ignore an item at its original price suddenly want it, not because they need it but because they feel they would be foolish to miss the opportunity. | 按原价时根本不会看一眼的购物者，突然就想要了，不是因为需要，而是因为觉得错过这个机会太蠢。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **not** | adv. | ★不；没有；并非 | Shoppers who would ignore an item at its original price suddenly want it, not because they need it but because they feel they would be foolish to miss the opportunity. | 按原价时根本不会看一眼的购物者，突然就想要了，不是因为需要，而是因为觉得错过这个机会太蠢。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **have** | v./aux. | ★有；使；吃；已经(构成完成时) | Nature reserves are usually drawn on maps as if the land were empty, but in most cases people have lived on it for generations. | 自然保护区在地图上画出来时，那片土地仿佛是空的，但在多数情况下，人们已经在那里世代居住。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **more** | adj./adv. | ★更多的；更；另外的 | Experiments show that customers are more likely to buy when they can see the comparison, even when the so-called original price was never actually charged. | 实验表明，只要能看到对照，顾客就更可能下单，哪怕那个所谓的原价其实从未真正收过。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **their** | pron. | ★他们的；她们的；它们的 | When a reserve is created, those residents may suddenly find that the activities which fed their families are now forbidden, while the animals they were asked to protect destroy their crops. | 保护区一设立，这些居民可能突然发现，养活一家人的营生现在被禁止了，而他们被要求保护的动物却毁了他们的庄稼。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **people** | n. | ★人们；人民；民族；国民；家人 | to the question "will I regret leaving it? People are not irrational to enjoy a bargain. | 人们喜欢捡便宜并不算不理性。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **with** | prep. | ★和…一起；具有；用；由于 | Conservationists sometimes regard such arrangements as a compromise with principle. | 环保人士有时认为这种安排是对原则的妥协。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **can** | v./aux. | ★能；可以；可能 | Experiments show that customers are more likely to buy when they can see the comparison, even when the so-called original price was never actually charged. | 实验表明，只要能看到对照，顾客就更可能下单，哪怕那个所谓的原价其实从未真正收过。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **from** | prep. | ★从；来自；由于 | Economists call this the transaction utility: the pleasure comes not from the object but from the bargain itself. | 经济学家称之为交易效用：快感不来自商品本身，而来自"捡了便宜"这件事。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **year** | n. | ★年；年份；学年；岁 | Where villagers are employed as guides, or where a proportion of the entrance fee is returned to the community, attitudes change within a few years, and local residents begin to report illegal hunting themselves. | 在村民被雇来做导游、或门票收入有一部分返还给社区的地方，几年之内态度就会转变，当地居民甚至会主动举报非法狩猎。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **this** | adj./pron. | ★这；这个；这样 | Economists call this the transaction utility: the pleasure comes not from the object but from the bargain itself. | 经济学家称之为交易效用：快感不来自商品本身，而来自"捡了便宜"这件事。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **were** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去式复数) | Nature reserves are usually drawn on maps as if the land were empty, but in most cases people have lived on it for generations. | 自然保护区在地图上画出来时，那片土地仿佛是空的，但在多数情况下，人们已经在那里世代居住。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **when** | adv./conj. | ★什么时候；当…时；虽然 | Experiments show that customers are more likely to buy when they can see the comparison, even when the so-called original price was never actually charged. | 实验表明，只要能看到对照，顾客就更可能下单，哪怕那个所谓的原价其实从未真正收过。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **was** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去式单数) | Experiments show that customers are more likely to buy when they can see the comparison, even when the so-called original price was never actually charged. | 实验表明，只要能看到对照，顾客就更可能下单，哪怕那个所谓的原价其实从未真正收过。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **which** | pron./adj. | ★哪一个；…的那个；关系代词 | Retailers understand the mechanism perfectly, which is why the original price is almost always displayed beside the reduced one. | 零售商对这套机制了如指掌，所以降价旁边几乎总会标出原价。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **because** | conj. | ★因为；由于 | Shoppers who would ignore an item at its original price suddenly want it, not because they need it but because they feel they would be foolish to miss the opportunity. | 按原价时根本不会看一眼的购物者，突然就想要了，不是因为需要，而是因为觉得错过这个机会太蠢。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **find** | v./n. | ★找到；发现；认为；感到；裁决；发现物 | When a reserve is created, those residents may suddenly find that the activities which fed their families are now forbidden, while the animals they were asked to protect destroy their crops. | 保护区一设立，这些居民可能突然发现，养活一家人的营生现在被禁止了，而他们被要求保护的动物却毁了他们的庄稼。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Passage Two |

### 选词填空（44 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **help** | v./n. | ★帮助；有助于；帮手 | Lecturers can help by dividing large projects into parts that must be submitted separately, which removes the possibility of leaving everything until the last week. | 任课教师可以帮忙把大项目拆成必须分次提交的部分，这样就没可能把一切都拖到最后一周。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |
| **write** | v. | ★写,书写；写作；写信 | Researchers have found that the most effective remedy is not a stricter schedule but a smaller first step: students who were asked merely to open the document and write one sentence frequently continued for an hour. | 研究人员发现，最有效的补救办法不是更严格的日程，而是更小的第一步：那些只被要求打开文档写一句话的学生，往往会接着写上整整一个小时。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |
| **large** | adj. | ★大的；大量的；广泛的 | Lecturers can help by dividing large projects into parts that must be submitted separately, which removes the possibility of leaving everything until the last week. | 任课教师可以帮忙把大项目拆成必须分次提交的部分，这样就没可能把一切都拖到最后一周。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |
| **schedule** | n./v. | ★时间表,日程；安排；时刻表；清单 | Researchers have found that the most effective remedy is not a stricter schedule but a smaller first step: students who were asked merely to open the document and write one sentence frequently continued for an hour. | 研究人员发现，最有效的补救办法不是更严格的日程，而是更小的第一步：那些只被要求打开文档写一句话的学生，往往会接着写上整整一个小时。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |
| **first** | adj./adv. | ★第一的；首先；最初的 | Researchers have found that the most effective remedy is not a stricter schedule but a smaller first step: students who were asked merely to open the document and write one sentence frequently continued for an hour. | 研究人员发现，最有效的补救办法不是更严格的日程，而是更小的第一步：那些只被要求打开文档写一句话的学生，往往会接着写上整整一个小时。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |
| **usually** | adv. | ★通常,经常 | Students who delay an assignment are not usually lazy; they are avoiding the unpleasant feeling that the task produces. | 拖延作业的学生通常并不懒，他们是在逃避这项任务带来的不愉快感受。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Procrastination |

### 长篇阅读（31 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **shop** | n./v. | ★商店；购物；车间 | Online platforms have removed the inconvenience that once made second-hand shopping slow and uncertain, since buyers can now compare prices and check the reputation of a seller within seconds. | 线上平台消除了过去让二手交易又慢又没把握的不便，因为买家现在几秒钟内就能比价、查看卖家的信誉。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **system** | n. | ★系统；制度；体系；方法 | The main obstacle remains trust, and platforms that offer reliable systems for returning goods have grown far faster than those that do not. | 主要障碍仍然是信任，而那些提供可靠退货机制的平台，发展速度远远快于不提供的平台。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **once** | adv./conj. | ★一次；曾经；一旦 | Buying second-hand goods was once regarded as a sign that a family could not afford new things. | 买二手货曾经被看作是一个家庭买不起新东西的标志。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **since** | conj./prep./adv. | ★因为,既然；自从…以来；此后 | Online platforms have removed the inconvenience that once made second-hand shopping slow and uncertain, since buyers can now compare prices and check the reputation of a seller within seconds. | 线上平台消除了过去让二手交易又慢又没把握的不便，因为买家现在几秒钟内就能比价、查看卖家的信誉。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **well** | adv./adj./int./n./v. | ★好,令人满意地；完全地；详细地；健康的；良好的；井；源泉；涌出 | Environmental concerns play a part as well: customers who would not describe themselves as activists still feel uneasy about throwing away objects that others could use. | 环保顾虑也起了作用：那些不会自称环保人士的顾客，仍然对把别人还能用的东西扔掉感到不安。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **into** | prep. | ★进入；到…里；变成 | Sellers, for their part, discover that items occupying space in their flats can be converted into cash. | 对卖家来说，他们发现占着自家地方的东西可以换成现金。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |

| **necessary** | adj. | ★必要的；必需的 | Attitudes have changed with remarkable speed, and young consumers in particular now describe it as a sensible choice rather than a necessary one. | 人们的态度变化之快令人瞩目，尤其是年轻消费者，如今他们把买二手货描述为明智的选择，而不是无奈之举。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **trust** | n./v. | ★信任,信赖；委托；托拉斯；相信；希望；确信 | The main obstacle remains trust, and platforms that offer reliable systems for returning goods have grown far faster than those that do not. | 主要障碍仍然是信任，而那些提供可靠退货机制的平台，发展速度远远快于不提供的平台。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **obstacle** | n. | ★障碍,障碍物；干扰；妨碍 | The main obstacle remains trust, and platforms that offer reliable systems for returning goods have grown far faster than those that do not. | 主要障碍仍然是信任，而那些提供可靠退货机制的平台，发展速度远远快于不提供的平台。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **sensible** | adj. | ★明智的,合理的；察觉到的 | Attitudes have changed with remarkable speed, and young consumers in particular now describe it as a sensible choice rather than a necessary one. | 人们的态度变化之快令人瞩目，尤其是年轻消费者，如今他们把买二手货描述为明智的选择，而不是无奈之举。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **occupy** | v. | ★占据；占用；使忙于；居住 | Sellers, for their part, discover that items occupying space in their flats can be converted into cash. | 对卖家来说，他们发现占着自家地方的东西可以换成现金。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **reliable** | adj. | ★可靠的,可信赖的 | The main obstacle remains trust, and platforms that offer reliable systems for returning goods have grown far faster than those that do not. | 主要障碍仍然是信任，而那些提供可靠退货机制的平台，发展速度远远快于不提供的平台。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **remarkable** | adj. | ★显著的,非凡的,值得注意的 | Attitudes have changed with remarkable speed, and young consumers in particular now describe it as a sensible choice rather than a necessary one. | 人们的态度变化之快令人瞩目，尤其是年轻消费者，如今他们把买二手货描述为明智的选择，而不是无奈之举。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |
| **uneasy** | adj. | ★不安的,担忧的；不自在的；不稳定的 | Environmental concerns play a part as well: customers who would not describe themselves as activists still feel uneasy about throwing away objects that others could use. | 环保顾虑也起了作用：那些不会自称环保人士的顾客，仍然对把别人还能用的东西扔掉感到不安。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · Second-hand Markets |

| **phone** | n./v. | ★电话,手机；听筒；打电话 | A secondary school has introduced a rule requiring students to leave their phones in a locked box at the start of each lesson, and teachers report a noticeable improvement. | 一所中学推行了一项新规定，要求学生每节课开始前把手机放进一个上锁的盒子里，教师反映情况有明显改善。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · News Report One |
| **staff** | n./v. | ★员工,全体职员；行政人员；为…配备人员 | According to the head teacher, the measure was not intended to punish anyone; it was introduced after staff observed that pupils were unable to concentrate for more than a few minutes. | 据校长说，这项措施本意不是惩罚谁，而是在教师发现学生无法集中注意力超过几分钟之后才推出的。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **lesson** | n. | ★课；教训；课程 | A secondary school has introduced a rule requiring students to leave their phones in a locked box at the start of each lesson, and teachers report a noticeable improvement. | 一所中学推行了一项新规定，要求学生每节课开始前把手机放进一个上锁的盒子里，教师反映情况有明显改善。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · News Report One |

| **chinese** | adj./n. | ★中国的；中文的；中国人；汉语 | Traditional Chinese medicine has a history of several thousand years. | 中医有几千年的历史。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **traditional** | adj. | ★传统的；惯例的 | Traditional Chinese medicine has a history of several thousand years. | 中医有几千年的历史。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **recent** | adj. | ★最近的,近来的 | In recent years, traditional Chinese medicine has attracted attention in other countries, although its methods are sometimes questioned by scientists who demand stricter evidence. | 近年来，中医在其他国家引起了关注，尽管它的方法有时会受到要求更严格证据的科学家的质疑。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **medicine** | n. | ★药；医学；内科 | Traditional Chinese medicine has a history of several thousand years. | 中医有几千年的历史。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **demand** | n./v. | ★需求；要求 | In recent years, traditional Chinese medicine has attracted attention in other countries, although its methods are sometimes questioned by scientists who demand stricter evidence. | 近年来，中医在其他国家引起了关注，尽管它的方法有时会受到要求更严格证据的科学家的质疑。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **attention** | n. | ★注意,注意力；照料；关注 | In recent years, traditional Chinese medicine has attracted attention in other countries, although its methods are sometimes questioned by scientists who demand stricter evidence. | 近年来，中医在其他国家引起了关注，尽管它的方法有时会受到要求更严格证据的科学家的质疑。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **doctors** | n. | ★医生(doctor 的复数) | Doctors examine the patient carefully and may use herbal medicine, massage or acupuncture. | 医生会仔细诊察病人，可能使用草药、推拿或针灸。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **sometimes** | adv. | ★有时；间或 | In recent years, traditional Chinese medicine has attracted attention in other countries, although its methods are sometimes questioned by scientists who demand stricter evidence. | 近年来，中医在其他国家引起了关注，尽管它的方法有时会受到要求更严格证据的科学家的质疑。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **balance** | n./v. | ★平衡；余额；使平衡 | It treats the human body as a whole, and it emphasises the balance between different parts of the system rather than a single symptom. | 它把人体当作一个整体来看待，强调系统各部分之间的平衡，而不是只盯着某一个症状。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **history** | n. | ★历史；来历；病史 | Traditional Chinese medicine has a history of several thousand years. | 中医有几千年的历史。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **method** | n. | ★方法,办法；条理 | In recent years, traditional Chinese medicine has attracted attention in other countries, although its methods are sometimes questioned by scientists who demand stricter evidence. | 近年来，中医在其他国家引起了关注，尽管它的方法有时会受到要求更严格证据的科学家的质疑。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **symptom** | n. | ★症状；征兆,表征 | It treats the human body as a whole, and it emphasises the balance between different parts of the system rather than a single symptom. | 它把人体当作一个整体来看待，强调系统各部分之间的平衡，而不是只盯着某一个症状。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |
| **dismiss** | v. | ★解散；解雇；不予考虑 | Supporters argue that it offers a different way of understanding health, and that it should be studied rather than dismissed. | 支持者认为，中医提供了理解健康的另一种方式，应当加以研究，而不该一味否定。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 中医 |

### 写作（13 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **think** | v. | ★认为,觉得；思考；想起 | For this reason I think schools should teach students how to analyse their mistakes instead of simply recording them. | 正因如此，我认为学校应当教学生如何分析错误，而不只是把错误记录下来。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **our** | pron. | ★我们的 | When everything goes well, we rarely examine our method, because the result seems to prove that the method was correct. | 一切顺利时，我们很少去检视自己的方法，因为结果似乎已经证明方法是对的。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **analyse** | v. | ★分析；解析 | For this reason I think schools should teach students how to analyse their mistakes instead of simply recording them. | 正因如此，我认为学校应当教学生如何分析错误，而不只是把错误记录下来。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **eventually** | adv. | ★最终,终于 | Nobody enjoys failure, and yet most of what we eventually learn comes from it. | 没有人喜欢失败，然而我们最终学到的大部分东西都来自失败。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **look** | v./n. | ★看；看起来；神情 | Failure removes that comfort and forces us to look for the step where things went wrong. | 失败拿走了这份安慰，逼我们去找究竟哪一步出了错。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **things** | n. | ★东西；事情(thing 的复数) | Failure removes that comfort and forces us to look for the step where things went wrong. | 失败拿走了这份安慰，逼我们去找究竟哪一步出了错。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **wrong** | adj./adv./n. | ★错误的,不正确的；不道德的；有毛病的；错误地；不公正；冤枉 | Failure removes that comfort and forces us to look for the step where things went wrong. | 失败拿走了这份安慰，逼我们去找究竟哪一步出了错。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **everything** | pron. | ★一切；所有事物 | When everything goes well, we rarely examine our method, because the result seems to prove that the method was correct. | 一切顺利时，我们很少去检视自己的方法，因为结果似乎已经证明方法是对的。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **nobody** | pron. | ★没有人；无人 | Nobody enjoys failure, and yet most of what we eventually learn comes from it. | 没有人喜欢失败，然而我们最终学到的大部分东西都来自失败。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **step** | n./v. | ★步骤；台阶；措施；踏 | Failure removes that comfort and forces us to look for the step where things went wrong. | 失败拿走了这份安慰，逼我们去找究竟哪一步出了错。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **strategy** | n. | ★策略,战略；策划 | Students who believe that ability is fixed treat a bad mark as evidence about themselves and give up; students who believe that ability can be developed treat the same mark as information about their strategy and try again. | 相信能力是天生的学生，把一个坏分数当作关于自身的证据，于是放弃；相信能力可以培养的学生，把同一个分数当作关于策略的信息，于是再试一次。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **go** | v. | ★去；变得；进行；运转 | When everything goes well, we rarely examine our method, because the result seems to prove that the method was correct. | 一切顺利时，我们很少去检视自己的方法，因为结果似乎已经证明方法是对的。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **painful** | adj. | ★痛苦的；费力的 | This is painful, but it is also honest. | 这个过程很痛苦，但也很诚实。 | 2021年12月 第1套 · 范文 |

## 2021年6月


### 仔细阅读（169 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **they** | pron. | ★他们；她们；它们；人们 | Every year, families argue about the same question: should a student choose the subject they love, or the one that leads to a secure job? | 每年，家家户户都在争论同一个问题：学生该选自己喜欢的专业，还是选好就业的那个？ | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **for** | prep./conj. | ★为了；因为；给；持续 | The problem cannot be solved by deleting rumours alone, because the demand for surprising stories remains. | 仅靠删除谣言解决不了问题，因为对离奇故事的需求还在。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **are** | v./aux. | ★是；存在(be 的复数) | The explanation is not that people are foolish. | 解释并不是人们愚蠢。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **who** | pron. | ★谁；…的人；他/她 | Graduates who selected a subject mainly because of its employment prospects are not necessarily happier in their work, and a substantial proportion of them leave the field within five years. | 主要因为就业前景而选择专业的毕业生，工作时未必更开心，其中相当大一部分人在五年内就离开了这个领域。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **not** | adv. | ★不；没有；并非 | The explanation is not that people are foolish. | 解释并不是人们愚蠢。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **have** | v./aux. | ★有；使；吃；已经(构成完成时) | Platforms have tried various responses, from warnings to removing content, but the most effective measure appears to be slower: teaching users to check the source before sharing. | 平台尝试过各种应对手段，从贴警告到下架内容，但最有效的办法似乎更慢：教用户在转发前先核实来源。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **has** | v./aux. | ★有(have 的第三人称单数) | Interest, meanwhile, has a practical value that is easy to overlook: students who are genuinely curious study harder, absorb material faster, and are more willing to persist when a course becomes difficult. | 与此同时，兴趣有一种容易被忽视的实用价值：真正好奇的学生学得更投入、吸收更快，也更愿意在课程变难时坚持下去。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **than** | conj./prep. | ★比；与…相比 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **more** | adj./adv. | ★更多的；更；另外的 | It is that a rumour is usually more surprising than the truth, and surprise is what makes something worth passing on. | 而是谣言通常比真相更让人意外，而意外正是值得转发出去的东西。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **their** | pron. | ★他们的；她们的；它们的 | Graduates who selected a subject mainly because of its employment prospects are not necessarily happier in their work, and a substantial proportion of them leave the field within five years. | 主要因为就业前景而选择专业的毕业生，工作时未必更开心，其中相当大一部分人在五年内就离开了这个领域。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **student** | n. | ★学生；大学生；研究者 | Every year, families argue about the same question: should a student choose the subject they love, or the one that leads to a secure job? | 每年，家家户户都在争论同一个问题：学生该选自己喜欢的专业，还是选好就业的那个？ | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **people** | n. | ★人们；人民；民族；国民；家人 | The explanation is not that people are foolish. | 解释并不是人们愚蠢。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **with** | prep. | ★和…一起；具有；用；由于 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **them** | pron. | ★他们；她们；它们 | People share material that expresses emotion, and rumours tend to be written in emotional language, whereas corrections are sober and careful, which makes them far less attractive. | 人们会转发带有情绪的内容，而谣言往往用情绪化的语言写成，相比之下辟谣文字克制而谨慎，吸引力就差得多。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **can** | v./aux. | ★能；可以；可能 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **from** | prep. | ★从；来自；由于 | Platforms have tried various responses, from warnings to removing content, but the most effective measure appears to be slower: teaching users to check the source before sharing. | 平台尝试过各种应对手段，从贴警告到下架内容，但最有效的办法似乎更慢：教用户在转发前先核实来源。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **year** | n. | ★年；年份；学年；岁 | Every year, families argue about the same question: should a student choose the subject they love, or the one that leads to a secure job? | 每年，家家户户都在争论同一个问题：学生该选自己喜欢的专业，还是选好就业的那个？ | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **this** | adj./pron. | ★这；这个；这样 | Researchers measured this by comparing how novel each message was, and found that falsehoods were consistently rated as more unexpected. | 研究人员通过衡量每条信息的新奇程度测出了这一点，发现虚假信息一贯被评为更出人意料。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **were** | v./aux. | ★是(be 的过去式复数) | Researchers measured this by comparing how novel each message was, and found that falsehoods were consistently rated as more unexpected. | 研究人员通过衡量每条信息的新奇程度测出了这一点，发现虚假信息一贯被评为更出人意料。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **when** | adv./conj. | ★什么时候；当…时；虽然 | Interest, meanwhile, has a practical value that is easy to overlook: students who are genuinely curious study harder, absorb material faster, and are more willing to persist when a course becomes difficult. | 与此同时，兴趣有一种容易被忽视的实用价值：真正好奇的学生学得更投入、吸收更快，也更愿意在课程变难时坚持下去。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **become** | v. | ★变成,成为；变得；适合 | Interest, meanwhile, has a practical value that is easy to overlook: students who are genuinely curious study harder, absorb material faster, and are more willing to persist when a course becomes difficult. | 与此同时，兴趣有一种容易被忽视的实用价值：真正好奇的学生学得更投入、吸收更快，也更愿意在课程变难时坚持下去。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **which** | pron./adj. | ★哪一个；…的那个；关系代词 | People share material that expresses emotion, and rumours tend to be written in emotional language, whereas corrections are sober and careful, which makes them far less attractive. | 人们会转发带有情绪的内容，而谣言往往用情绪化的语言写成，相比之下辟谣文字克制而谨慎，吸引力就差得多。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **treat** | v./n. | ★对待,看待；处理；治疗；请客；款待；乐事 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **evidence** | n. | ★证据,迹象 | The debate is usually presented as a choice between idealism and realism, but the evidence does not support such a sharp distinction. | 这场争论通常被描述为理想主义与现实主义之间的选择，但证据并不支持如此泾渭分明的划分。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **information** | n. | ★信息,资料；情报；通知 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **remove** | v. | ★移走,去掉；脱下；免职；搬家；消除 | Platforms have tried various responses, from warnings to removing content, but the most effective measure appears to be slower: teaching users to check the source before sharing. | 平台尝试过各种应对手段，从贴警告到下架内容，但最有效的办法似乎更慢：教用户在转发前先核实来源。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **share** | v./n. | ★分享,共享；分担；股份；份额；一份 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **support** | v./n. | ★支持,支撑；供养；证实；支持；支撑物；赡养 | The debate is usually presented as a choice between idealism and realism, but the evidence does not support such a sharp distinction. | 这场争论通常被描述为理想主义与现实主义之间的选择，但证据并不支持如此泾渭分明的划分。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **usually** | adv. | ★通常,经常 | It is that a rumour is usually more surprising than the truth, and surprise is what makes something worth passing on. | 而是谣言通常比真相更让人意外，而意外正是值得转发出去的东西。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **value** | n./v. | ★价值；价格；重要性；重视；评价 | Interest, meanwhile, has a practical value that is easy to overlook: students who are genuinely curious study harder, absorb material faster, and are more willing to persist when a course becomes difficult. | 与此同时，兴趣有一种容易被忽视的实用价值：真正好奇的学生学得更投入、吸收更快，也更愿意在课程变难时坚持下去。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **within** | prep. | ★在…之内；在…里面；不超过；在内部 | Graduates who selected a subject mainly because of its employment prospects are not necessarily happier in their work, and a substantial proportion of them leave the field within five years. | 主要因为就业前景而选择专业的毕业生，工作时未必更开心，其中相当大一部分人在五年内就离开了这个领域。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **between** | prep. | ★在…之间；在…中间 | The debate is usually presented as a choice between idealism and realism, but the evidence does not support such a sharp distinction. | 这场争论通常被描述为理想主义与现实主义之间的选择，但证据并不支持如此泾渭分明的划分。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **clear** | adj./v. | ★清楚的；明显的；清除；晴朗的 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **produce** | v./n. | ★生产；产生；拿出；农产品 | A story about an ordinary event produces no desire to share it, while a story that seems incredible demands an audience. | 一件平常事不会产生分享的欲望，而一个听起来不可思议的故事却非要有人听不可。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **ask** | v. | ★问；要求；请求 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **best** | adj./adv. | ★最好的；最好地 | The students who do best are rarely those who followed a formula. | 表现最好的学生，很少是那些照着公式选的人。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **compare** | v. | ★比较,对比；比喻 | Researchers measured this by comparing how novel each message was, and found that falsehoods were consistently rated as more unexpected. | 研究人员通过衡量每条信息的新奇程度测出了这一点，发现虚假信息一贯被评为更出人意料。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **family** | n. | ★家庭；家人；家族；子女 | Every year, families argue about the same question: should a student choose the subject they love, or the one that leads to a secure job? | 每年，家家户户都在争论同一个问题：学生该选自己喜欢的专业，还是选好就业的那个？ | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **language** | n. | ★语言；语言文字；措辞；风格 | People share material that expresses emotion, and rumours tend to be written in emotional language, whereas corrections are sober and careful, which makes them far less attractive. | 人们会转发带有情绪的内容，而谣言往往用情绪化的语言写成，相比之下辟谣文字克制而谨慎，吸引力就差得多。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **ordinary** | adj. | ★普通的,平常的；平凡的；平庸的；常规的；一般的 | A story about an ordinary event produces no desire to share it, while a story that seems incredible demands an audience. | 一件平常事不会产生分享的欲望，而一个听起来不可思议的故事却非要有人听不可。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **something** | pron. | ★某事；某物；大约 | It is that a rumour is usually more surprising than the truth, and surprise is what makes something worth passing on. | 而是谣言通常比真相更让人意外，而意外正是值得转发出去的东西。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **alone** | adj./adv. | ★独自的；仅仅；单独地 | The problem cannot be solved by deleting rumours alone, because the demand for surprising stories remains. | 仅靠删除谣言解决不了问题，因为对离奇故事的需求还在。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **factor** | n. | ★因素,要素 | There is a second factor. | 还有第二个因素。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **millions** | n. | ★数百万；大量 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |

| **accurate** | adj. | ★准确的,精确的；正确无误的 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **audience** | n. | ★观众；听众；读者 | A story about an ordinary event produces no desire to share it, while a story that seems incredible demands an audience. | 一件平常事不会产生分享的欲望，而一个听起来不可思议的故事却非要有人听不可。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **foolish** | adj. | ★愚蠢的；傻的 | The explanation is not that people are foolish. | 解释并不是人们愚蠢。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **market** | n./v. | ★市场；集市；需求；销售；推销 | That said, it would be dishonest to ignore the market. | 话虽如此，无视市场也不诚实。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |
| **rate** | n./v. | ★比率,率；速度；价格；评估；定级 | Researchers measured this by comparing how novel each message was, and found that falsehoods were consistently rated as more unexpected. | 研究人员通过衡量每条信息的新奇程度测出了这一点，发现虚假信息一贯被评为更出人意料。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **simple** | adj. | ★简单的；朴素的；单纯的 | Studies suggest that even a brief pause, prompted by a simple question, reduces the number of false stories people forward. | 研究表明，哪怕只是由一个简单问题引发的短暂停顿，就能减少人们转发的假消息数量。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **tried** | v. | ★尝试(try 的过去式)；努力 | Platforms have tried various responses, from warnings to removing content, but the most effective measure appears to be slower: teaching users to check the source before sharing. | 平台尝试过各种应对手段，从贴警告到下架内容，但最有效的办法似乎更慢：教用户在转发前先核实来源。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **uncomfortable** | adj. | ★不舒服的；不自在的 | A study that examined millions of messages shared online reached an uncomfortable conclusion: false information travels faster, further and deeper than accurate information. | 一项研究了数百万条网络转发信息的研究，得出了一个令人不安的结论：虚假信息比真实信息传得更快、更远、更深。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
