| **user** | n. | ★用户,使用者；吸毒者 | Platforms have tried various responses, from warnings to removing content, but the most effective measure appears to be slower: teaching users to check the source before sharing. | 平台尝试过各种应对手段，从贴警告到下架内容，但最有效的办法似乎更慢：教用户在转发前先核实来源。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage One |
| **combine** | v. | ★结合,联合 | A sensible approach is to treat employment data as information rather than as an instruction, and to ask how a subject can be combined with skills that employers value, such as writing clearly, analysing data or speaking another language. | 明智的做法是把就业数据当作信息而不是指令，并思考如何把一个专业与雇主看重的能力结合起来，比如写得清楚、会分析数据或会说一门外语。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Passage Two |

| **sharply** | adv. | ★急剧地；严厉地；尖锐地；鲜明地；突然地 | The rapid expansion of food delivery has created a large workforce whose conditions differ sharply from those of traditional employees. | 外卖行业的迅速扩张造就了一支庞大的劳动队伍，他们的工作条件与传统雇员截然不同。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · Gig Work and Delivery Riders |

### 翻译（13 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **praise** | n./v. | ★赞扬,表扬；赞美 | Parents teach these rules to their children from an early age, and visitors who observe them are usually praised for their manners. | 父母在孩子很小的时候就教这些规矩，而遵守这些规矩的客人通常会被称赞有教养。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 筷子与餐桌礼仪 |
| **china** | n. | ★中国；瓷器 | Chopsticks have been used in China for at least three thousand years, and they reflect a practical attitude towards food: dishes are cut into small pieces before they are served, so a knife at the table is unnecessary. | 筷子在中国至少已经使用了三千年，它反映出一种务实的饮食态度：菜在上桌前就切成了小块，因此餐桌上不需要刀。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 筷子与餐桌礼仪 |
| **example** | n. | ★例子；榜样；范例 | For example, they should not be stood upright in a bowl of rice, because this resembles the incense burned for the dead, and it is considered unlucky. | 比如，不能把筷子竖着插在饭碗里，因为这像为逝者所燃的香，被认为不吉利。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 筷子与餐桌礼仪 |

| **chopsticks** | n. | ★筷子 | Chopsticks have been used in China for at least three thousand years, and they reflect a practical attitude towards food: dishes are cut into small pieces before they are served, so a knife at the table is unnecessary. | 筷子在中国至少已经使用了三千年，它反映出一种务实的饮食态度：菜在上桌前就切成了小块，因此餐桌上不需要刀。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 筷子与餐桌礼仪 |

### 写作（11 词）

| 单词 | 词性 | 释义 | 真题原句 | 原句译文 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **cash** | n./v. | ★现金；兑现 | Electronic payment has become so common that some people predict the end of cash. | 电子支付已经普遍到有人预言现金将要消失。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **payment** | n. | ★支付；付款；报酬 | Electronic payment has become so common that some people predict the end of cash. | 电子支付已经普遍到有人预言现金将要消失。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **common** | adj. | ★常见的；共同的；普通的；平常的 | Electronic payment has become so common that some people predict the end of cash. | 电子支付已经普遍到有人预言现金将要消失。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **convenient** | adj. | ★方便的,便利的；近便的；合适的；省事的 | It is certainly convenient: a transaction takes seconds, there is no risk of receiving the wrong change, and every purchase is recorded automatically. | 它确实方便：一笔交易几秒钟完成，不存在找错钱的风险，每一笔消费还被自动记录下来。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **elderly** | adj. | ★年长的；上了年纪的 | Many elderly people still find digital payment confusing, and they would be excluded from ordinary shopping if notes and coins were abolished. | 许多老年人仍然觉得数字支付难以理解，如果纸币和硬币被废除，他们连日常购物都会被排除在外。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **option** | n. | ★选择,选项；选修课；期权 | For these reasons, cash should remain an option rather than a rarity, and shops that accept only cards or phones should be required to say so at the entrance. | 基于这些理由，现金应当保留为一种选项，而不是变成稀罕物；只接受刷卡或手机支付的商店，应当在门口明示这一点。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **predict** | v. | ★预测,预言 | Electronic payment has become so common that some people predict the end of cash. | 电子支付已经普遍到有人预言现金将要消失。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **confuse** | v. | ★使困惑；混淆 | Many elderly people still find digital payment confusing, and they would be excluded from ordinary shopping if notes and coins were abolished. | 许多老年人仍然觉得数字支付难以理解，如果纸币和硬币被废除，他们连日常购物都会被排除在外。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **purchase** | v./n. | ★购买；购买物；紧握 | It is certainly convenient: a transaction takes seconds, there is no risk of receiving the wrong change, and every purchase is recorded automatically. | 它确实方便：一笔交易几秒钟完成，不存在找错钱的风险，每一笔消费还被自动记录下来。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **privacy** | n. | ★隐私,私生活；独处 | There is also the question of privacy, since electronic records reveal a great deal about a person's habits. | 还有隐私的问题，因为电子记录会透露一个人大量的生活习惯。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
| **transaction** | n. | ★交易,业务；办理；处理；事务 | It is certainly convenient: a transaction takes seconds, there is no risk of receiving the wrong change, and every purchase is recorded automatically. | 它确实方便：一笔交易几秒钟完成，不存在找错钱的风险，每一笔消费还被自动记录下来。 | 2021年6月 第1套 · 范文 |
